### Page 22

spending; the bottom 60%, who spend roughly as many dollars as
the top 20%, are squeezed by tariﬀs and higher fuel prices. Second,
the Fed has less room to cut than in 2001. Third, the credit cushion
is thinner: rising delinquencies on car loans and credit cards show
stress building at lower incomes.
The good news: this is not 2008. US household debt stands at
around 70% of GDP, the same as in 2001 and well below the 100%
reached in 2007.
In the end, the wealth eﬀect is the smaller channel. After the

dotcom bust, business investment fell by 11% in two years. IT
equipment was the least of it, falling just 6%, transport equipment
fell 21% and manufacturing buildings plunged by a whopping
55%. Tech may light the fuse, but the explosion happens elsewhere.
What would a tech sell-off mean for the
dollar and FX markets?
Not necessarily the classic safe-haven dollar rally – this time, a
tech sell-oﬀ could well hurt the dollar.
The usual playbook says risk-oﬀ means dollar up. But an AI-led

correction would be a US-centric shock. Foreign investors hold large,
partly unhedged positions in US equities, built up over a decade of
American outperformance. A sell-oﬀ in US tech could trigger
repatriation and higher hedge ratios, both dollar-negative – as in
the 'sell America' episode of April 2025. The IMF estimates the
dollar would fall by around 6% in an AI-led equity correction.
The rates channel points in the same direction. Negative wealth

eﬀects, weaker consumption and slower US growth would
eventually push the Fed towards rate cuts, narrowing interest rate
diﬀerentials against the dollar. The timing would depend on
inﬂation: with prices still running above target, the Fed may cut
later than markets hope, which could limit the dollar's fall in the
early stages.
Among the winners, the yen and the Swiss franc would play their
traditional safe-haven role. The euro could beneﬁt relatively, given

Europe's lower exposure to both AI capex and equity wealth (4Q). A

### Page 23

tech crash made in America would, for once, be a reason to hold
European assets rather than ﬂee them.
The losers would be the currencies of Asian tech exporters, notably
the Taiwan dollar and the Korean won, whose economies are
leveraged bets on AI demand. They would come under heavy
pressure, although intervention by local authorities and the legacy
of huge trade surpluses would cushion the blow somewhat.
The bottom line: in an AI bust, the dollar may lose its reputation as

the place to hide. Not because the world has found a better safe
haven, but because this time the ﬁre would start in the safe house.
Our base case for 2027: boom, plateau or
bust, and which signposts should readers
watch?
Our base case is a plateau at altitude: the boom continues in 2027,
but its contribution to growth peaks. A bust remains the main risk
scenario.
Early indicators point to tight semiconductor supply into 2028,
suggesting the investment cycle still has legs. But as capex growth

slows, AI's contribution to US growth should fade.
The diﬀerence between the two scenarios matters enormously for
the US. A plateau would give the non-tech economy room to
breathe: capital, energy and workers no longer absorbed by AI
could ﬂow back, allowing non-tech investment – and potentially
employment – to rebound. A bust would be a very diﬀerent animal:
an abrupt choking oﬀ of tech investment, similar to 2001,
combined with a negative wealth eﬀect on consumers through

falling stock markets. And this time, central banks would have less
room for rate cuts as inﬂation remains their main concern.
Which signposts should readers watch? For the boom itself, the key
indicators are hyperscaler capex guidance, Taiwanese order books
and lead times, and AI revenues relative to capex. Markets will focus
on realised AI revenues at the large technology ﬁrms, margins on
incremental revenue and how the next phase of the build-out will

On ﬁnancing, credit spreads on AI-related debt, private credit ﬂows

and redemptions, and bond yields and term premia will show
whether investors remain willing to fund the build-out. In the real
economy, the questions are whether non-tech investment ﬁnally
recovers, how hiring in AI-exposed sectors develops, and what
productivity, grid connection and power data are telling us.
The question for 2027 isn't whether AI will change the world. It's
whether anyone gets paid for it in time. If earnings fail to match
sky-high expectations, the plateau could turn out to be a ledge.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 360: Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 16
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports
Europe’s auto deal with China is a good start. But containing China’s export boom
will require a much broader response.
European Commissioner for Trade and Economic Security Maros Sefcovic speaks at a press briefing at the EU Delegation to
China in Beijing, China October 9, 2026. Florence Lo/Reuters
PUBLISHED
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
November 2, 2026 11:02 a.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The year-over-year increase in Chinese automobile
exports to all markets is currently about 3.5 million
cars.
Over ten years, that is 35 million cars. The global
market excluding China is only around 60 million
cars. Japan raised its auto exports by about 3.5 million
cars in the 1970s—but that was over a decade, not
one year. And right now the U.S. and Indian markets

(around 20 million cars) are effectively closed to
Chinese production. The 2026 jump in exports is an
incredible.
China’s domestic market, though, is down 20 percent
this year—that works out to a decline of about 5
million cars. The Wall Street Journal estimates that
domestic sales will fall to around 19 million cars this
year, less than a third of the production capacity of
China’s auto industry.
So even though China’s exports are increasing at an

unprecedented pace, China’s domestic auto sector
isn’t really booming; production is essentially flat, and
intense domestic competition is destroying the
profitability of firms that don’t export.*

### Page 3

3
Europe’s new auto deal with China should be
understood in this context.
The deal limits China’s exports of hybrids (including
plug-in hybrids) to Europe to around 350,000 cars.
That is above the 300,000 hybrids that China
exported to Europe in 2025, but well below the (gulp)
800,000 annualized pace for hybrids in the last three
months of (European) data. Total imports,
annualized, were running at an incredible 2 million a
year pace thanks to strong battery electric imports in

September.

### Page 4

4
China appears to have concluded that it prefers a
guaranteed market for a limited number of cars (and
more or less guaranteed profits) to the risk of a
prohibitive tariff that would cut off all its exports.
And Europe preferred a negotiated deal to the risk of
retaliation and a slowdown in licensing rare earth and
magnet exports.
The Commission also thinks of this as a bit of a
model—more on that later.
I certainly understand the logic of the deal.

Something needed to be done. China’s export of
hybrids to Europe were up an insane 200 percent
year-over-year in the last 3 months of data, which led
to a 75 percent increase for all cars. One more year of
75 percent growth would bring China’s exports to

### Page 5

5
around 3 million cars; in two years, close to 6 million,
and in three years, 10 million—or most of the market.
But Europe still is a long way from a coherent trade
and industrial policy with respect to autos, let alone a
coherent economic policy towards overall trade with
China.
After this deal, as I understand it:
Hybrid imports are capped at 350,000, down
from a projected 800,000 in 2026—and then
rising to 370,000 and 390,000 in subsequent
years.

Battery electric vehicles face a tariff from 25
percent (Telsa, 10 percent base + 15 percent) to
45 percent (10 percent base plus 35 percent).
BYD’s total tariff is at 27 percent, which isn’t
prohibitive. Even with these tariffs, battery
electric imports will total around 600,000 this
year, up from 500,000 last year.
Diesel and gasoline (ICE) cars face a tariff of
10 percent. Europe is importing around
200,000 of these a year (including a significant

number from Chinese-owned MG).
There are no special tariffs on imported parts or
imported batteries, which matters for the growing
number of Chinese factories in Europe. So long as
tariffs are low and the renminbi is undervalued,
Chinese firms still have an incentive to maximize the

### Page 6

6
Chinese content of their “transplant” factories (ask
Thailand).
There is currently no unified European policy on
inward investment that requires joint ventures or
technology transfer either, only a lot of wishful
thinking.**
Sum it up, and it is a market where Chinese exports
are constrained by a hodge-podge of different trade
defense tools, and there is no standard policy for
ensuring that Chinese assembly operations use
European parts rather than import Chinese kits. The

existing operations, which are just starting to scale up,
don’t draw on a European supply chain. They likely
preserve a few assembly jobs at the expense of the
broader automotive ecosystem.
A coherent European (or national) industrial policy
for the automotive and battery sectors is thus still a
work in progress.
The safeguard on hybrids and the resulting tariff rate
quota only stops the bleeding.
And make no mistake, Europe’s position in the global

automotive sector is slipping. Europe still runs an
overall surplus in auto trade, but that surplus is falling
fast if trade in batteries is added to automotive trade
(there isn’t a split between batteries used for energy

### Page 7

7
storage and batteries used by cars in the EU data
right now, at least not that I have found).
Europe’s broader trade policy to China is similarly a
work in progress.
The surge in imports of hybrid cars was particularly
pronounced, but it is by no means the only such surge.
The balance in industrial machinery has eroded
significantly. Consider the following chart, which
shows imports and exports of machinery, electronics
and vehicles (HS 84, 85, and 87) by weight.

### Page 8

8
The balance in chemicals, measured by weight to
abstract from price changes, shows the same trend.
Europe doesn’t have an across-the-board tool for
applying tariffs against China like the U.S. section
301 statute.

### Page 9

9
But that isn’t necessarily an immediate constraint, as
there are plenty of solid safeguard cases thanks to the
current surge in European imports from China.
But if each safeguard needs to include a negotiated
tariff-rate quota that gives China enough guaranteed
market access (and Chinese firms enough guaranteed
profits) to ensure trade peace, Europe needs to get
moving with negotiation in other sectors.
Chemicals and machinery are more diverse sectors

than cars and thus aren’t as amenable to the same
high-level Š ef č ovi č -Wang negotiations the led to the
most recent deal.
This critique isn’t meant as criticism. Europe needed
to act on cars. Look at a chart of automotive trade in
kilos.

### Page 10

10
It’s just that it also needs to act in other sectors: auto
parts, batteries (though that is complicated),
chemicals, and some categories of industrial
machinery. The scale of China’s export boom and the
fact that in most sectors there are no limits to China’s
ability to scale up is a real problem.***
And Europe cannot wait for China to conclude that
it would rather grow on the basis of internal, rather
than external, demand.
I disagree with the IMF’s former top brass on this. A

China that grows on the back of a 1.5 pp net export
contribution for another couple of years will
deindustrialize much of the world. China’s trading
partners cannot step aside and do nothing until
China figures out how to escape deflation using its
own demand. Xi appears more than happy to
continue with his current policy mix and gamble that

### Page 11

11
the world will continue to absorb China’s ever
growing export surplus.
Pascal Lamy, Nicolas Köhler-Suzuki, and Victor Luiz
do Prado of the Jacques Delors Institute recognize
the need for a more systemic response—as do Jean-
Pierre Landau and Sébastien Jean, who have done an
excellent job explaining how China’s micro- and
macro-industrial policies fit together into a coherent,
self-reinforcing whole.
The proposals in the Lamy et al. paper overlap heavily

with the Commission’s current approach to China.
The authors envision a two-pronged approach: first,
negotiating with China to seek agreement on tariff-
rate quotas that effectively limit the EU’s imports
from China; and second, developing a broad yet
targeted tariff-based instrument to address structural
imbalances should negotiations fail. The latter would
give Europe a credible threat should China refuse to
agree to the proposed safeguards.
My own view: don’t let the perfect be the enemy of

the good.
The EU has shied away from developing its own 301
tool out of concern about preserving a “rules-based
system.” I still think a European 301 would allow a
more fulsome response, but understand that
European isn’t as willing as the United States to use

### Page 12

12
tools that at least appear to be outside those
authorized by the WTO. In reality the EU no longer
really wants to trade with China on the same (MFN)
terms applied to other WTO members. That mirrors
the current view of the United States. But the EU is
willing to go through more procedural hoops to get to
a similar result.****
But I also recognize that, absent policy adjustment in
China—notably a stronger currency that allows for
fair market competition—the scope of safeguards and

managed trade will progressively expand.
* One sign: Stellantis is resuming Jeep production in
China, but primarily for export rather than the
domestic market.
** There is a lot of (in my view) somewhat confused
analysis of Chinese investment that works off the
analogy provided by either foreign investment in
China’s auto sector or Japanese investment in the U.S.
and Europe in the 1980s. These are relevant examples
of course, but asserting that Chinese investment will

result in a similar outcome when the underlying
economics differ is a big logical jump. Japanese firms
had incentives to localize their parts production in the
United States from the then-strong yen as well as
from the risk of protection. Foreign firms operating in
China had to do JVs until recently and had incentives
to localize from China’s significant tariff on auto

### Page 13

13
parts and China’s lower cost structure (in part because
of a weak RMB). Tesla’s Shanghai incentives and tax
deal included strong localization incentives, and there
were additional incentives from Tesla’s desire to
qualify for the lists the governed access to China’s
consumer EV subsidies. That is why something like
90 percent of Tesla parts, including its batteries, are
now made in China. Chinese investors in Europe
have no similar incentives to localize right now, yet

many assume that the results will be the same. That,
to me, is naïve. Since the underlying economics differ
(Europe’s cost basis is much higher than existing
production in China) getting Chinese firms to do
more than final assembly in Europe will take stronger
policy tools than were required to get foreign firms to
localize parts production in China. Europe’s
Industrial Accelerator Act would be a step in the
right direction, but more is likely needed. Thailand

provides a useful cautionary example.
*** China can make over 60 million cars, according to
recent reporting from the Wall Street Journal. The
domestic market this year will be under 20 million
cars (down from something like 24 million cars last
year). Even with exports of 10 million, there is ~30
million cars worth of unused auto production
capacity (roughly double European demand). And
China is still investing at a pace that is consistent
with adding close to 5 million of EV production

capacity a year. Chinese firms, who don’t make any
money on domestic sales, are strongly incentivized to

### Page 14

14
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The
Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.
From Follow the Money and Greenberg Center for Geoeconomics
export. The world has never encountered such elastic
supply.
**** The more different the legal basis for the
European and U.S. response—and the more policy
differences (TRQs vs. a flat tariff, for example) the

harder it would be to ever develop a harmonized
policy approach. But with President Trump, a
harmonized policy isn’t even a U.S. goal.

### Page 15

The Week Ahead October 30, 2026

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank Negara Malaysia (BNM) (5 
November)

As written in our Macro Strategy note, 
BNM’s final meeting of 2026 on 5 
November will be live and a close call, 
after the central bank opened the door to 
monetary policy normalisation during its 
September review. BNM Governor Abdul 
Rasheed Ghaffour reiterated in a 
Bloomberg interview on 15 October that 
the central bank has space to adjust 
policy if needed, as policymakers are 
vigilant about upside inflationary 
pressures amid strong economic growth. 
He also said that policy decisions will be 
data dependent. We think the latest 
available September data for the 
upcoming meeting suggest that 
policymakers can afford to be patient 
until 1H27, maintaining the Overnight 
Policy Rate at 2.75% for now. Inflation 
remained contained within the lower half 
of the official 2026 inflation forecast 
range of 1.5-2.5%, notwithstanding 
external price pressures and robust 
economic activity. Advance estimates 
showed GDP growth of 5.3% yoy in 3Q26, 
while exports jumped to a five-year high 
of 54.5% yoy in September, 
supercharged by the artificial 
intelligence boom. However, headline 
inflation eased slightly to 1.8% yoy in 
September 2026 from 1.9% yoy in August, 
while core inflation remained stable at 1.7% 
yoy. Fuel subsidies have limited the pass-
through of upstream cost pressures to 
consumer prices, alongside stable 
domestic demand conditions.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

South Korea

October trade and inflation data are due 
for release. Export growth is expected to 
accelerate to nearly 100% yoy, up from 
83.7% in September, while the trade 
surplus is forecast to remain above 
USD40bn for another month. CPI 
inflation is expected to hold steady at 2.9% 
yoy. Overall, the data should reinforce 
the view that AI- and semiconductor-
driven growth and the expansion in 
South Korea’s trade surplus remain 
firmly on track, providing fundamental 
support for the KOSPI and KRW. 
Meanwhile, inflation remains sticky 
above the BOK’s 2% target, bolstering 
the case for further monetary tightening 
as early as the November policy meeting.

Taiwan

October inflation data are forthcoming. 
Headline CPI inflation is expected to 
remain above 2% for the sixth 
consecutive month, at 2.3% yoy. Core 
CPI inflation is also likely to stay above 2% 
for the sixth consecutive month, at 2.3% 
yoy. Despite government subsidies on 
fuel and electricity prices, higher global 
energy prices continue to feed through 
to CPI via items such as airfares and tour 
package fees. Services inflation also 
remains sticky, particularly in food away 
from home, housing, transportation, and 
entertainment. Meanwhile, domestic 
inflation expectations appear to be 
gradually rising. A recent private-sector 
survey showed that the public expects

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 30, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

inflation to average 2.5% in 2026, with 
52% of respondents expecting inflation 
to exceed 2.5%. Against this backdrop, 
we maintain our view that the central 
bank will resume rate hikes in December, 
raising the policy rate by 12.5 bps to 
2.125%.

Thailand

We expect Thailand’s headline inflation 
to rise to 3.2% yoy in October 2026, 
extending the increase over the previous 
two months, and moving slightly above 
the upper end of the Bank of Thailand’s 
1-3% target range. This was likely driven 
by faster energy inflation amid an 
uptrend in diesel prices, alongside a 
rebound in food inflation. The inflation 
uptrend was likely broadly in line with 
the central bank's expectations and 
mainly supply-side driven, supporting its 
decision to look through the temporary 
price pressures and keep its policy rate 
unchanged at its October meeting.

Vietnam

We expect Vietnam’s October 2026 
goods exports and retail sales to remain 
robust at 42.0% yoy and 14.0% yoy year-
to-date, respectively, while headline 
inflation is likely to stay elevated at 
around 5.0% yoy. Exports have 
continued to be supported by strong 
electronics and machinery shipments 
amid a favourable technology cycle with 
spillovers from robust artificial 
intelligence-related demand. Retail 
sales were likely bolstered by firm 
household spending, supported by 
favourable labour market conditions, 
and continued strength in tourism. 
Headline inflation has remained elevated 
and above the central bank’s target of 
4.5% for the year, driven by price 
pressures from transport and energy, 
food, as well as material costs. This 
poses a challenge for policymakers as 
they seek to balance inflation risks 
against ambitions of achieving at least 10% 
economic growth this year.

Hong Kong

Hong Kong’s economy is expected to 
decelerate from 4.3% yoy in Q2 to 2.6% 
in Q3, largely due to slower private 
consumption. Despite a 13% increase in 
Mainland visitor arrivals during the 
period, domestic consumption continued 
to be weighed down by a weakening 
labour market, with the unemployment 
rate elevated at 3.9% in September. 
Meanwhile, higher interest rates have 
dampened investment sentiment and 
capital market activity. The Centa-City 
Leading Index remained broadly flat, 
while the Hang Seng Index was range-
bound at around 25,000. The positive 
wealth effect seen in 1H has faded as 
asset market performance remained 
muted. A strong HKD continued to 
encourage outbound tourism, evidenced 
by an increase in average daily resident 
departures from 6.9% yoy in Q2 to 10.9% 
in Q3.

However, GDP growth is expected to be 
buffered by improving net exports amid 
the ongoing AI-electronics upcycle. 
Export growth picked up and outpaced 
import growth during Q3. Hong Kong, as 
China’s free port and re-export hub, is 
benefiting from stronger demand for 
Chinese electronic goods.

Indonesia/ Philippines

### Page 4

The Week Ahead October 30, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         4

Indonesia’s 3Q GDP likely moderated to 
5.2% y/y. Soft consumer confidence and 
retail sales growth point to weaker 
consumption, compounded by a gradual 
rise in inflation. Fiscal measures likely 
helped spending among low-income 
households. Despite the quarterly 
moderation, we maintain our full-year 
growth forecast at 5.3% y/y.

Inflation rates in Indonesia and the 
Philippines are expected to rise, albeit to 
different degrees. Indonesia’s inflation is 
on course to edge up modestly, while in 
the Philippines, stronger pass-through 
from higher oil prices, food and 
transportation costs, as well as imported 
inflation pressures stemming from a 
weaker peso, is likely to push inflation to 
a multi-year high.

Document 440: Time To Sound The All Clear Signal_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Time To Sound The All Clear Signal_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Time To Sound The All Clear Signal?
Date:    6 hours ago
Time To Sound The All Clear Signal?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Time To Sound The All Clear
Signal?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 11 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Guille Álvarez / Unsplash

I. From Caution to Buying Opportunities
On September 15, we advised our readers to "Proceed With Caution." In
that note, we pushed our year-end S&P 500 target of 8,400 to mid-2027
and lowered our 2026 target to 7,900. Our concern wasn't earnings. We
maintained our 2027 S&P 500 EPS forecast of $425 but lowered our year-
end forward P/E forecast from 19.8 to 18.6.

### Page 2

Why? Our worry list was getting longer: geopolitical conflicts, rising bond
yields, domestic political uncertainty, and concerns about the AI trade. We
reckoned that these risks were increasing and would depress valuations
(FOMO) despite the ongoing strength of the economy and fabulous
earnings momentum (FEMO).
We reiterated our caution on October 14. On October 24, we observed
that buying opportunities were starting to emerge, but better ones were

likely after the midterms. On November 2, we warned that a Democratic
sweep could deliver a cold shower for Wall Street. The Nasdaq plunged
on November 5. That evening's QT was titled, "We Promised You Buying
Opportunities After The Midterms." We concluded that Thursday's selloff
was the buying opportunity we had anticipated.
Our post-election cold shower warning proved timely. After initially rallying
on Wednesday following Tuesday's midterm elections on November 3, the

S&P 500 sold off sharply on Thursday. It found support at its 200-day
moving average and has rebounded since then (chart). It is only 2.6%
below its October 5 record high. The S&P 500 equal weight index is back
at its record high.
The Nasdaq rose to a record high on October 6 (chart). It then plunged
11.9% into correction territory through November 5. It is up 4.7% since
then.

### Page 3

FEMO has offset FOMO. Soaring S&P 500 forward earnings has offset
the drop in the forward P/E (charts). We think that the latter probably
bottomed on November 5, while the former should continue rising, albeit
at a slower pace.

### Page 4

Our worry list is getting shorter:
(1) Geopolitics. The feared post-midterm re-escalation of the Middle East
war did not materialize. Instead, President Donald Trump adopted a more
conciliatory tone after Republican election losses, which some
Republicans partly blamed on the unpopular war. Correspondingly, oil
prices have retreated recently (chart). The White House now seems
determined to maintain the status quo by continuing to blockade Iran,
while seeking a diplomatic solution.

(2) Bond yield. The 10-year Treasury yield is back in our old normal
range of 4.00%-5.00% (charts). We view this range as consistent with
solid economic growth. It rebounded to the top of the range yesterday
after October's hotter-than-expected CPI, which increased the odds of a

### Page 5

25bps Fed rate hike early next month. We've expected a 50bps hike ever
since the last FOMC meeting, which voted against another hike.
(3) Domestic policies. The midterms are done, removing one key source
of uncertainty. The market's recent rebound suggests investors are
adjusting to the new political landscape, though fiscal standoffs over the
federal budget and debt ceiling remain a risk. In our opinion, the Blue
Wave in Congress and state governorships might slow data center

construction, but it won't stop the AI buildout (chart).

### Page 6

(4) AI investment boom. Investors seem to be coming around to our view
that financial, political, and physical constraints should be speed bumps,
not roadblocks, to the AI buildout. That's principally because demand for
computing power remains exceptionally strong. CoreWeave's earnings
report yesterday provides further evidence, with Q3 revenue surging
168% y/y to $3.7 billion and backlog reaching $132.5 billion. Such robust
demand not only justifies continued investment in computing capacity but

also helps ease financing concerns. Indeed, the company expects roughly
$9 billion in customer prepayments and deposits, reducing its reliance on
expensive borrowing.
But is this exceptionally strong demand for computing power sustainable?
The latest results from OpenAI and Anthropic suggest that it is. OpenAI's
Q3 revenue surged 160% y/y to $10 billion, while Anthropic's soared
1,127% to almost $17 billion. This exponential revenue growth provides

compelling evidence of rapidly expanding demand for AI services, which
will continue to fuel compute demand. Meanwhile, reports that Anthropic's
IPO is already multiple times oversubscribed suggest that investors
remain eager to finance the boom.
II. Bearish Sentiment Is Bullish
We are pleased to see that the Investors Intelligence Bull/Bear Ratio
plunged to 1.54 last week (chart). We expected that to happen in
response to last Thursday's panic attack. From a contrarian perspective,

this is a strong buy signal.

### Page 7

For the remainder of the year, we expect analysts to moderate their
earnings forecasts as they account for the speed bumps ahead, while
investors become more comfortable paying a higher multiple as earnings
estimates become more credible and sentiment improves.

Document 470: Will earnings resolve the bond-equity split_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Will earnings resolve the bond-equity split_.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Weekly Market Views)
Date: 2026-10-09
Pages: 15
Status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office 
9 October 2026

Will earnings resolve 
the bond-equity split?

 Bond and equity market volatility has 
sharply diverged. Elevated bond 
volatility and political risks in the US 
and Europe warrant near-term caution, 
prompting us to lock in tactical gains in 
semiconductors and Taiwan equities. 
 Our quantitative stock-bond model 
turned negative for the first time since 
March 2025, signalling near-term equity 
volatility. We stay invested in a 
diversified allocation slightly overweight 
equities and prefer to navigate the 
upcoming results and macro events 
before adding back high-beta exposure 
within equities. 
 Consensus estimates point to a 31% 
y/y rise in S&P500 Q3 earnings, driven 
by technology-sector earnings growth 
of 67%. Earnings have beaten lofty 
estimates in H1 as the AI build-out 
exceeds expectations. This favours 
tech-led equities in the medium term.  
 In Europe, French political and fiscal 
risks cloud the EUR outlook, but 
regional bank equities remain attractive

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Charts of the week: Diverging volatility

Strong earnings should resolve the bond and equity market divergence; French political risks remain largely contained  
S&P500 volatility index (VIX); US bond volatility index (MOVE)*   10-year government bond yield spreads over German bonds

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *measures US government bond volatility

Editorial 
Will earnings resolve the bond-equity split?  
Strategy summary: Bond and equity market volatility has 
sharply diverged. Elevated bond volatility and political risks in 
the US and Europe warrant near-term caution, prompting us to 
lock in tactical gains in semiconductors and Taiwan equities. 
Our quantitative stock-bond model turned negative for the first 
time since March 2025, signalling near-term equity volatility. 
We stay invested in a diversified allocation slightly overweight 
equities and prefer to navigate the upcoming results and macro 
events before adding high-beta exposure within equities. 
Consensus estimates point to a c. 31% y/y rise in S&P500 Q3 
earnings, driven by technology-sector earnings growth of c. 
67%. Earnings have beaten lofty estimates in H1 as the AI 
build-out exceeds expectations. This favours tech-led equities 
in the medium term. In Europe, French political and fiscal risks 
cloud the EUR outlook, but regional bank equities remain 
attractive on robust profitability, capital strength and valuations. 
Divergence between equities and bonds. Equity markets 
signal calm, with the S&P500 index near record highs and its 
volatility index (VIX) low at 15, while the US government bond 
volatility index (MOVE) is close to its highest since March. Bond 
stress reflects two-decade-high long yields amid strong growth, 
oil close to USD 100/bbl fuelling near-term inflation, fiscal 
uncertainty and crowded investor short positioning vulnerable 
to abrupt unwinds. High bond volatility could spill into equities. 
Our quantitative stock-bond model turns negative. For the 
first time since March 2025, the model score has turned 
negative, driven by deteriorating equity market breadth and 
above-average risk aversion. History shows global equity 
volatility rises most in the month after the score dips to -1. 
However, equities still outperformed bonds over the following 
1-12 months, on average, despite higher market risk. 
Taking some chips off the table; locking in gains: Rising 
bond volatility, the negative stock-bond model signal, weak 
equity breadth and approaching US mid-term elections warrant 
near-term caution. US small-caps remain about 9% below 
August’s all-time highs even as large-caps reach new records. 
The macro uncertainty prompted us to lock in gains in 
semiconductor and Taiwan equity opportunistic ideas. 
Strong Q3 earnings season likely to overcome near-term 
challenges. Strong US and Asian earnings should ultimately 
resolve the equity-bond divergence in favour of stocks. 
Consensus S&P500 Q3 earnings growth has risen to 30.6% 
y/y, while technology is tracking 66.5%, driven by the AI 
infrastructure buildout. AI investment plans, monetisation and 
funding needs are key metrics to watch. Record memory-chip 
profits and higher long-term targets from AI leaders support 
staying long technology-led risk assets and using near-term 
volatility to scale into preferred sectors. Also, US equities have 
historically performed strongly in the 12 months after mid-term 
elections, particularly when the opposition regains control of the 
House and the Senate – as seems increasingly likely. 
Medium-term constructive on equities. Beyond Q3 earnings, 
we expect US earnings to rise 15-20% next year. Almost half of 
the estimated USD 420 per share of S&P500 earnings in 2027 
should come from tech-related sectors: information technology, 
communication services and consumer discretionary. We also 
favour US, Europe and Japan financial sectors as solid growth 
and higher rates lift lending income, while the AI buildout boosts 
advisory fees from IPOs and M&As. However, another 30-
50bps rise in long-term bond yields, without earnings growth 
upgrades, could be a headwind for equities. Next week’s US 
inflation data for September is the next catalyst for bond yields. 
French uncertainty challenges near-term EUR view; 
continue to see opportunities in Euro area bank equities. 
Rising French political and fiscal risks have largely remained 
confined to France, with its bond yield spread versus German 
peers surging to the widest since the 2011-12 Euro area crisis. 
Nevertheless, EUR/USD has fallen around 4% since mid-
August, and our three-month forecast of 1.15 is under review. 
Tighter financial conditions and political uncertainty in France 
and Spain raises the risk of an ECB rate hold. Euro area bank 
equities remain attractive on profitability, capital strength and 
valuations, subject to sovereign-spread and funding-cost risks.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the market implications of France's political and 
fiscal uncertainty?   
Our view: France’s political and fiscal uncertainty remain near-
term headwinds for European assets. We are placing our three-
month EUR/USD forecast (1.15) under review for a potential 
downward adjustment, while retaining our 12-month forecast at 
1.18 as broader Euro area spillovers remain limited. Our base 
case remains one more ECB hike in December followed by a 
prolonged pause, although tighter financial conditions increase 
the risk of a delay. For equities, we retain Euro area banks as 
an Opportunistic idea, supported by strong profitability, capital 
buffers and reasonable valuations. For bank bonds, we prefer a 
subordinated capital structure to senior debt. 
Rationale: France’s 10-year government bond yield has risen amid 
renewed political and fiscal concerns. This has tightened financial 
conditions and added to the near-term EUR risk premium. Together 
with elevated US yields, this raises downside risks to our three-
month EUR/USD view. However, the stress has not yet developed 
into broad Euro area contagion, and recent expectations of progress 
on France’s 2027 budget have provided some relief. Broader Euro 
area growth and labour market fundamentals also remain relatively 
resilient, supporting our unchanged 12-month EUR/USD view. 
For the ECB, higher sovereign yields are already delivering some 
monetary tightening. Markets have consequently pared expectations 
of additional ECB hikes. We continue to expect one more hike in 
December, given elevated near-term inflation, but persistent fiscal 
or political stress in France and Spain could delay further tightening. 
For Euro area banks, wider sovereign spreads are likely to raise 
funding costs and pressure bond portfolios, while fewer ECB hikes 
would moderate further gains in net interest income. Nevertheless, 
profitability remains strong, capital buffers are healthy and price-
to-book valuations remain reasonable, with returns on equity also 
improving. We, therefore, maintain Euro area banks as an 
Opportunistic idea, while monitoring sovereign spreads, funding 
costs and any deterioration in loan-loss provisions.  
On the bond side, European bank junior subordinated bond prices 
fell by an average of 3-5 cash points before rebounding, with French 
bank subordinated bonds underperforming those of other European 
peers. As fundamentals remain strong, we view pullbacks as 
opportunities to add and maintain our preference for a subordinated 
capital structure over senior debt for European bank bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist  
 Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the likelihood of elevated long-end government 
bond yields evolving into a systemic risk for the financial sector 
and public finances? 
Our view: We believe systemic risk remains a tail risk rather 
than a base case in the near term. The probability would rise 
materially if rollover stress, foreign demand withdrawal and 
private credit deterioration occur in combination. 
Rationale: Higher bond yields worsen fiscal deficits and drive higher 
issuance requirements, exerting further upward pressure on yields. 
The resulting tighter financial conditions weigh on economic growth. 
This feedback loop is most acute in Developed Markets (DMs) where 
debt burdens are already elevated. For instance, projected 2026 
debt-to-GDP ratios stand at 204% for Japan, 138% for Italy, 126% 
for the US, 118% for France and 104% for the UK. Countries with 
shorter average debt maturities face the sharpest near-term 
repricing risk as maturing obligations are refinanced at current 
rates. Meanwhile, evidence suggests that a positive equity-bond 
correlation increases the sensitivity of yields to bond supply.  
Taking the US as an example, there has been no buyers’ strike on 
US government bonds yet, though foreign demand has softened, 
narrowing the margin of safety. The US government has shifted 
towards shorter-dated issuance to manage funding costs (reducing 
near-term expenses, but concentrating rollover risk). Economic 
resilience remains evident, underpinned by corporate confidence 
in AI-driven investment. The key question is whether this 
expansion can persist long enough to stabilise the fiscal trajectory. 
Within the financial sector, corporate balance sheets remain 
strong, while High-Yield (HY) bond and leveraged loan default rates 
remain contained, though this reflects the lag between rate rises and 
credit deterioration, not immunity. Following the failure of the Silicon 
Valley Bank, liquidity discipline among banks has improved, but 
duration mismatches persist.  
In private credit, default rates are trending upward while the asset 
class has come under continued scrutiny, with negative headlines 
and a rise in redemptions triggering fund gating at some private 
credit managers since Q1 2026. Hence, continued monitoring of 
default rates, redemptions and potential stress events in private 
credit remains essential. That said, higher rates could buoy 
medium-term returns and distribution yields in the asset class, 
as the majority of direct lending loans have a floating-rate structure. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

Most G8 economies have government debt-to-GDP 
ratios exceeding 100%

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for the US Q3 2026 earnings season, 
especially for the banking sector? 
Our view: We expect another quarter of robust US earnings 
growth to support our Overweight stance on US equities. We 
see major US banks, which begin reporting next week, also 
delivering healthy results, reinforcing our Overweight view on 
the US financial sector. 
Rationale: Consensus forecasts S&P500 Q3 earnings growth of 
30.6% y/y, up from 27.6% at the start of July. Energy is expected to 
post the strongest growth at 114.7%, supported by elevated oil 
prices amid ongoing geopolitical tensions. Information technology 
(66.5%) and communication services (49.0%) remain key 
earnings drivers, benefiting from sustained AI-related demand and 
investment. The broader outlook remains supportive, with full-year 
earnings growth projected at 35.8% in 2026 and 15.6% in 2027. 
For banks, the recent underperformance amid concerns over higher 
long-term bond yields has, in our view, improved the forward-looking 
risk-reward profile. Over the medium term, strengthening loan 
growth and resilient credit quality should continue to support solid 
fundamentals. Capital markets activity also remains constructive, 
with industry leaders well-positioned to benefit from their strong 
investment banking and trading franchises. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst 
  How could upcoming semiconductor manufacturing and 
equipment earnings reshape the AI capex outlook? 
Our view: We maintain our AI capex outlook as robust demand 
and ongoing supply constraints support a multi-year expansion 
cycle. In the near term, however, we take a more prudent stance 
on semiconductors amid an expected rise in equity volatility. 
Rationale: Recent memory industry guidance points to sustained 
investment in advanced chip manufacturing, with customer 
commitments extending through 2031. Upcoming earnings should 
provide visibility into capacity expansion plans. This supports our AI 
capex outlook, which we will monitor through the earnings season.  
That said, we expect equity volatility to rise in the near term amid 
elevated long-term yields and macroeconomic uncertainty. Recent 
media reports on AI lab revenues have also led to doubts over 
monetisation and the ability to sustain elevated capex spending. We 
thus took profit on our Global Semiconductors (8.1% return from 9-
Sep-26 to 2-Oct-26) and MSCI Taiwan (10.0% return from 9-Jul-26 to 
2-Oct-26) Opportunistic ideas following strong performance. This does 
not reflect a deterioration in fundamentals, but rather a need to be 
prudent on high-beta exposure. We remain constructive on the AI 
theme over the long term. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  USD/CHF has rebounded sharply over the past two 
months. What are the key factors behind the move, and do you 
see the currency pair continuing to move higher? 
Our view: USD/CHF tested 0.84 amid strong momentum from 
the USD, but we see the pair remaining supported near-term 
before gradually moving lower towards 0.82 in three months. 
Rationale: The Fed’s relatively high policy rate continues to support 
USD carry demand, while the Swiss National Bank maintains its 
policy rate at 0%. In the near term, however, the Swiss franc (CHF) 
could benefit from increased safe-haven demand amid mounting 
fiscal concerns in France and political uncertainty in Spain, 
particularly if European risk premia continue to rise. Beyond these 
cyclical factors, Switzerland’s substantial net foreign asset position, 
persistent current account surpluses and low public debt burden 
continue to underpin the CHF’s structural strength. Over the 
medium term, we expect the USD’s rate advantage to gradually 
diminish as the Fed approaches the end of its tightening cycle, while 
the CHF’s defensive characteristics should remain supportive.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  Do you see further upside for USD/JPY after recent dovish 
comments from BoJ officials and latest bond auctions? 
Our view: We see some near-term upside risk to USD/JPY from 
elevated US yields, but retain our medium-term constructive 
JPY view. USD/JPY remains below 159, suggesting markets are 
mindful of intervention risk as the pair approaches 160. We 
continue to expect further BoJ normalisation. 
Rationale: Recent BoJ communication appears less dovish than the 
market narrative had suggested. BoJ Governor Ueda continues to 
emphasise the need to anchor underlying inflation around 2% 
and gradually reduce monetary accommodation, supporting our view 
that the tightening cycle is not yet over. An October pause would 
reflect a more measured pace of policy normalisation rather than a 
change in the BoJ’s policy direction. 
Recent Japanese government bond (JGB) auctions have shown 
resilient demand despite higher yields. At the latest 10-year JGB 
auction, the bid-to-cover ratio improved to 3.76x from 3.29x 
previously, while the auction tail (spread between highest 
accepted yield and issued yield) narrowed to 0.2bps from 1.6bps. 
We see this as indicative of an orderly, functioning market rather than 
a dovish BoJ signal. Intervention concerns around the 160 level 
should help limit near-term USD/JPY upside.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

《AI發債引發債務可持續性擔憂》
近期公債殖利率攀高引發市場對AI融資需求以及
企業償債能力的擔憂，AI產業鏈信用利差與CDS
擴大，不過多數大型科技企業多為投資級，美國
投資級AI產業鏈信用利差水準仍未突破今年高點；
此外統計今年以來大量發債的美國投資級債發行
人(淨發行量100億美元以上)共有17家，其中17%
的發行人信評獲調升，33%被下調，50%不變，
反映債務增加尚未造成普遍的債信壓力。

重點摘要 穆迪統計五大超大規模資料中心(微軟、Meta、谷
歌、亞馬遜、甲骨文)因獲利強勁，平均槓桿率為
2.1倍，而10年平均2.7倍，是自2013年以來的最低
水平，償債能力尚無疑慮；研判近期部分科技業利
差擴大主要反映市場對AI經濟效益的重新評估，以
及對評級較低、資本密集度較高的發行人要求更高
的風險溢酬，並非潛在的信用風險事件或整體信用
基本面惡化，不過仍須留意目前總體經濟環境對於
信用評級較低的發行人而言更容易受到長期高利率
和成本壓力的影響，高低評等發行人的市場表現分
歧或將加大。
另一方面，回顧2026年第三季美投資級債評等變動
不多，2026年迄今的評級上調/下調比率為2.3倍；
雖然這一比率低於2022-2024年期間強勁的升評趨

勢，但高於2025年，且已是第六年大於一倍，反映
投資級債整體信用環境維持良好。

重點摘要

狀況良好、資產品質未顯著
惡化
《歐洲信用債市受法國財政疑慮波及》
歐洲信用市場雖受AI相關債券波動影響較小，但因
能源價格高企，加上近期法國主權和財政風險加劇，
歐洲信用債市十月份表現也有不小波動，尤其是非
投資級債信用利差一度擴大逾60個基點。歐洲銀行
債是各產業信用債主權風險曝險較高的類別，因歐
洲銀行多數不及美國銀行營運遍及全球且業務多元，
此外也持有不少主權債資產，十月份市場表現也反
映此一狀況，主權債的波動更加重了歐洲銀行債的
跌幅，法國銀行債利差擴大幅度最為明顯，所幸法
國主要銀行包括法國巴黎銀行以及興業銀行3Q26獲
利狀況良好，即使經營環境惡化，從不良貸款率與

信用風險成本等資產品質指標來看法國巴黎銀行持
平前季、法國興業銀行僅微幅上升。
目前市場關注的焦點仍是法國財政整頓的路徑，法
國目前的債務負擔約佔GDP的118%至119%，高盛
預計，如果沒有更有效的財政調整，到2030年初負
債比率將進一步上升至接近125%，如要穩定債務
佔GDP的比重，法國需要實現約佔GDP 1%的財政
盈餘，但目前的財政赤字約佔2.5% GDP。法國政
府當局可能在年底前通過一項撙節的預算案，若沒
通過法國主權債與信用債可能進一步惡化，但即使
通過法國主權債利率要大幅回落的機會仍不大，可
能要到2027年法國大選後主權債才有機會趨於穩定，
屆時法國銀行債或有布局機會。
歐洲可供吸收損失銀行債信用利差(基點)
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

重點摘要
• 高利率持續下信用基本面都
已 出 現分 化，如CCC等級的
償債能力指標顯著疲弱
• 衡量整體債信風險仍是歐洲
較美國為高
《非投資級債美國仍優於歐洲》
儘管目前不論是美國還是歐洲的非投資級債在高利
率持續下信用基本面都已出現分化，如CCC等級的
償債能力指標顯著疲弱，但衡量整體債信風險仍是
歐洲較美國為高；原因包括歐洲的債務到期牆較美
國明顯，且美國非投資級債2028年前到期的債券分
佈在相對廣泛的行業，而當前的賤售債券主要集中
在媒體、原物料與電信、非必需消費等特定領域，
上述較少重疊降低落入賤售債券利差區間的發行人
同時面臨債務到期後償債或再融資令違約風險增加
的可能。另一方面，歐洲非投資級債的到期牆更為
陡峭，且市場利率與2028年前到期債券的票面利率

差距大於美非投資級債，反映再融資壓力更鉅，尤
其是歐洲CCC等級。
分析歐洲非投資級債各產業債務到期狀況可發現
2028年前到期債務產業相對集中，且易受景氣影響
的非必需消費在到期債務與賤售債券都佔有一定比
例，另外槓桿率本就較高的電信業佔2028年前到期
債務9%，融資成本大幅上漲恐令這些發行人債信體
質進一步惡化。
資料來源：高盛，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

產業聚焦降息前的美國汽車產業投資布局
2026/10/30
• 美國傳統車商迎來皮卡改款
週期
• 高油價使油電、電動車需求
提升
• 自駕車、軟體營收增加帶動
車商獲利
美國傳統車商迎來皮卡改款週期，獲利表現有望提升
美國傳統車廠近年轉向生產更多高價卡車和SUV，因部分SUV和皮卡的調整後息稅前利潤率高達20%、超
過其他車型，也使美國新車平均交易價格在9月超過5萬美元。福特與通用汽車即將在2026年年底至2027
年迎來全尺寸皮卡改款與更新計畫，由於在推出新款或新一代卡車後，通常會有2~3年較佳的銷售期，預
估福特和通用汽車在2027年及之後的獲利有機會持續提升。
美國政策轉為支持燃油車發展，但高油價使油電、電動車需求提升

儘管美國政策轉向支持燃油車發展，但自美伊戰爭後油價居高不下，使油電混合車3Q26在美國的市佔率
暴衝至16%，包括豐田、現代等品牌在美國的市占率持續上升。美國3Q26電動車銷量約佔新車總銷量
6.5%，高於2Q26的5.8%，顯示電動車在美國市場正重新站穩腳步。由於油價上升使全球對電動車需求
增加，特斯拉2Q26、3Q26交車量也優於市場預期。
自駕車接受度提升，軟體營收增加帶動車商獲利
美國消費者對自動駕駛的認識與接受度正持續提升，而各車商正積極擴大自駕與軟體訂閱服務布局。通用
汽車預估其OnStar和Super Cruise自駕輔助營收在2026年將突破30億美元，福特汽車的軟體付費訂閱用
戶已達160萬戶。3Q26特斯拉FSD活躍用戶數已達174萬、年增67%，且北美新車FSD採用率超過75%。
相較傳統車商低於10%的硬體銷售利潤率，軟體毛利率高達50~70%，且訂閱制使車商部分營收轉為持續
性收入，將帶動其估值表現。

美國9月份輕型車年化銷售量約為
1,600萬輛，低於市場預估共識的
1,630萬輛。儘管9月初的勞動節
促銷活動使購車回饋及零售銷售
增加，但同時銷售量年減4%也顯
示市場需求正面臨更大挑戰。
今年上半年儘管面臨高利率、高
車價等負面影響，但高收入族群
支撐使美國車市仍具韌性。然而
隨著利率上升、對消費者可負擔
性的懷疑日益增加，市場目前正
逐步將2026年美國輕型車銷售量
預估由1,610萬輛下修至1,560至
1,600萬輛區間。
聯準會在9月升息一碼，使美國
目標利率區間上升至3.75~4%，
且目前市場普遍預期聯準會在今
年12月及2027年上半年將再各升
息一碼。美國約有75~85%新車
購買使用貸款或融資，利率持續
上升將直接推高車貸利率，進而
增加消費者的購車成本與負債壓
力，且利率上升也會造成核貸門
檻轉趨嚴格，使消費者對新車的
需求下降。
未來若油價、通膨下降，市場重

新步入降息循環後有望使美國車
市需求重新回升。資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/9/24，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/22

福特、通用汽車全尺寸皮卡在改款當年及之後
兩年銷售表現優於同業
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，2026/10/13，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/22
車商將迎來皮卡改款週期
回饋優惠在交易價中佔比美國新車交易價格美國新車平均交易價格( ATP )在
9月超過5萬美元，車價上漲在每

年下半年為常見現象，以因應車
款更新而重新定價。然而，9月
ATP年增率低於1%遠低於典型的
9月增幅2~4%，顯示利率上升後
平價車款銷售相對強勁。
儘管美國新車售價成長幅度趨緩，
但其上升趨勢仍使美國傳統車商
利潤率逐步改善。通用、福特汽
車等傳統車廠近年轉向生產更多
卡車和SUV，因部分SUV和皮卡
的調整後息稅前利潤率超過20%，
使得車廠利潤率能維持一定水準。
(美元)
福特與通用汽車即將在2026年年
底至2027年迎來全尺寸皮卡改款
與 更 新 計 畫 ， 福 特 旗 艦 皮 卡 F-
150將換上全新3.0升V6引擎，通
用汽車則將對GMC Sierra 及
Chevrolet Silverado全尺寸皮卡
進行大改款。
鑑於福特與通用汽車在皮卡銷售
中取得的較高利潤，皮卡市場對
獲利的重要性可想而知。由於原
廠在推出新款或新一代卡車後，
通常會有2~3年較佳的銷售期，
預估福特和通用汽車在2027年及
之後的獲利有機會持續提升。

4
高油價使美國電動車銷售逐步回溫
資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28
需求使油電、電動車並存
康寧儘管政策轉向支持燃油車發展，
但自美伊戰爭後油價居高不下，
使油電混合車3Q26在美國的市佔
率暴衝至16%，包括豐田、現代
等品牌在美國的市占率持續上升。

而儘管美國3Q26電動車銷量較去
年同期下降(3Q25為電動車補助
期限，基期較高)，但仍較2Q26
成長近8%，約佔美國新車總銷量
的6.5%，高於2Q26的5.8%，顯
示電動車在美國市場正重新站穩
腳步。以市場反應來看，美國車
市正從先前單一電動化路線，轉
向燃油、油電、電動車並存局面。
美國國會在2025年9/30 正式取
消購買或租賃新電動車的7,500
美元稅收抵免，造成美國電動車
銷售在4Q25開始大幅下滑。另
外，川普政府在今年9月底正式
宣布放寬美國企業燃油效率標準，
將未來新車的燃油效率年均提升
目標下調，使部分車商開始降低
電動車生產以減少虧損。
傳統車企如福特、通用汽車的電
動車部門EBIT利潤率可能介於-
10~-30%以下，燃油效率標準放
寬使其生產方向轉回燃油車發展，
也有助於改善整體獲利結構。

5
通用汽車遞延收入上升，顯示未來軟體營收增加
資料來源：公司，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/29
軟體營收增加帶動獲利表現
各車商正積極擴大自駕與軟體訂閱
服務布局，並將其視為未來重要獲
利成長動能。 通 用 汽 車 預 估 其
OnStar和Super Cruise自駕輔助營

收在2026年將突破30億美元，並
在未來維持雙位數成長。福特汽車
的軟體付費訂閱用戶已達160萬戶。
3Q26特斯拉FSD活躍用戶數已達
174 萬 、 年增67% ， 且 北 美 新 車
FSD採用率超過75%。
相較傳統車商低於10%的硬體銷售
利潤率，軟體毛利率高達50~70%，
且訂閱制使車商部分營收轉為持續
性收入，將帶動其估值表現。
美國消費者對自動駕駛的認識與接
受度正持續提升。過去十年來，對
自駕車有較多了解的民眾比例由
40%增至58%，且近半數消費者相
信在有生之年將能看到自駕車普及。
另外，消費者雖然支持車輛自動化
發展，但仍傾向保有人工接管能力，
因此對Level 4 的 興 趣 最 高 ， 對
Level 5的興趣反而下降。
調查也發現，多數曾搭乘Robotaxi
的使用者對自動駕駛的觀感明顯改
善，未來隨著服務覆蓋範圍持續擴
大，Robotaxi有望進一步提升大眾
對自駕技術的信心與接受度。 資料來源：Cox Automotive，2026/9/28，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/29

Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 5

### Page 1

从环⽐看， 10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业增加值⽐上⽉增⻓ 0.21% 。 1—10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业
增加值同⽐增⻓ 5.3% 。分产品看，汽⻋产量下降 7.7% ，新能源汽⻋增⻓ 17.1% ， 3D 打印
设备、锂离⼦电池、⼯业机器⼈产品产量同⽐分别增⻓ 85.9% 、 30.0% 、 26.0% 。
摘要：
10 ⽉，⼯业⽣产较快增⻓，但较上⽉有所放缓，装备制造业和⾼技术制造业⽀撑明显。
据国家统计局 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇公布数据， 10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业增加值同⽐实际增⻓ 4.8% （增加值增
速均为扣除价格因素的实际增⻓率），前值为 5.5% 。从环⽐看， 10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业增加值⽐
上⽉增⻓ 0.21% 。 1—10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业增加值同⽐增⻓ 5.3% 。
分三⼤⻔类看， 10 ⽉份，采矿业增加值同⽐增⻓ 0.7% ，制造业增⻓ 5.4% ，电⼒、热⼒、燃⽓及
⽔⽣产和供应业增⻓ 4.4% 。
分经济类型看， 10 ⽉份，国有控股企业增加值同⽐增⻓ 2.1% ；股份制企业增⻓ 5.2% ，外商及港

澳台投资企业增⻓ 3.0% ；私营企业增⻓ 3.2% 。
分⾏业看， 10 ⽉份， 41 个⼤类⾏业中有 28 个⾏业增加值保持同⽐增⻓：
分产品看， 10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业 626 种产品中有 254 种产品产量同⽐增⻓。其中，钢材 11161
万吨，同⽐下降 5.1% ；⽔泥 14375 万吨，下降 1.3% ；⼗种有⾊⾦属 685 万吨，下降 1.2% ；⼄烯
356 万吨，增⻓ 1.1% ；汽⻋ 304.5 万辆，下降 7.7% ，其中新能源汽⻋ 202.7 万辆，增⻓ 17.1% ；
发电量 7940 亿千⽡时，下降 2.1% ；原油加⼯量 5830 万吨，下降 8.1% 。
3D 打印设备、锂离⼦电池、⼯业机器⼈产品产量同⽐分别增⻓ 85.9% 、 30.0% 、 26.0% 。
10 ⽉份，规模以上⼯业企业产品销售率为 96.5% ，同⽐下降 0.4 个百分点；规模以上⼯业企业实
现出⼝交货值 15580 亿元，同⽐名义增⻓ 18.6% 。
其中，煤炭开采和洗选业下降 2.9% ，⽯油和天然⽓开采业增⻓ 9.4% ，农副⻝品加⼯业增⻓

4.0% ，酒、饮料和精制茶制造业增⻓ 4.9% ，纺织业增⻓ 2.0% ，化学原料和化学制品制造业增⻓
1.3% ，⾮⾦属矿物制品业下降 2.3% ，⿊⾊⾦属冶炼和压延加⼯业下降 1.7% ，有⾊⾦属冶炼和压
延加⼯业下降 4.4% ，通⽤设备制造业增⻓ 8.5% ，专⽤设备制造业增⻓ 11.5% ，汽⻋制造业增⻓
0.9% ，铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业增⻓ 9.2% ，电⽓机械和器材制造业增⻓
8.1% ，计算机、通信和其他电⼦设备制造业增⻓ 19.4% ，电⼒、热⼒⽣产和供应业增⻓ 4.2% 。

Document 556: 中国景気は内需の弱さを背景に外需依存を深める展開が続く ～米中「貿易休戦」の延長は追い風も、巨額の貿易黒字が新たな摩擦を招く可能性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国景気は内需の弱さを背景に外需依存を深める展開が続く ～米中「貿易休戦」の延長は追い風も、巨額の貿易黒字が新たな摩擦を招く可能性～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年11 月 10 日

1/3 
中国景気は内需の弱さを背景に外需依存を深める展開が続く 
～米中「貿易休戦」の延長は追い風も、巨額の貿易黒字が新たな摩擦を招く可能性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 中国政府は９日、米国からの輸入品に対する 24％の追加関税の適用停止措置を２ヵ月間延長
すると発表した。重要鉱物の輸出規制や米国企業への制裁、船舶に対する特別港湾料などの
停止措置も延長し、９月の米中首脳会談での合意を履行する姿勢を示した。背景には、今月
の中国・深圳での APEC 会議や来月の米国・マイアミでのＧ20 会議に合わせた首脳会談を控
え、米中間の協議継続に向けた時間を確保するとともに、内需が力強さを欠くなかで景気を
下支えする外需の安定を維持したいとの思惑があると考えられる。 
○ こうしたなか、中国の 10 月の輸出額は前年比＋35.2％と高い伸びを維持した。米欧向けに加
え、アジア新興国や中南米、アフリカ向けも堅調に推移しており、世界的なＡＩ・半導体需
要の拡大などが追い風となっている。一方、輸入額も同＋41.6％と加速しており、ハイテク
関連の需要拡大や資源価格の高止まりが押し上げ要因となった。この結果、10 月の貿易黒字
は 1052.8 億ドルとなり、年初来の累計黒字は前年より１ヵ月早く１兆ドルを突破した。巨額
の貿易黒字は人民元相場の安定に寄与する一方、人民元高による輸出競争力の低下が懸念さ
れるほか、貿易不均衡を巡る国際的な批判が再燃する可能性もある。中国当局は、外需への
依存が強まるなかで、人民元相場の安定と対外貿易摩擦への対応という難しい政策運営を迫
られると見込まれる。

【中国と米国は貿易休戦を２ヵ月間延長、中国景気は外需への依存を強める展開が続く】 
中国国務院（関税税則委員会）は９日、10 日午後１時１分（北京時間）に期限を迎える、米国か
らの輸入品を対象とする 24％の追加関税の適用停止期限について、２ヵ月間延長すると発表した。
この発表に合わせて、商務部は、一部の重要鉱物と関連技術に対する輸出規制の適用除外について、
２ヵ月（2027 年１月 10 日まで）延長すると発表した。また、「信用できないエンティティ―リス
ト」に掲載された11 社、輸出管理リストに掲載された 16 社に対する措置停止のほか、ハンファオ
ーシャンの米国関連子会社５社に対する制裁停止措置も同様に２ヵ月延長することを明らかにし
た。加えて、交通運輸部も、米国関連の船舶に対する特別港湾料の徴収停止措置を２ヵ月間延長し、
海運・造船に関連するサプライチェーンの安全保障関連調査の停止も継続することを明らかにした。

### Page 2

2/3 
一連の措置について、国務院は、９月にワシントンＤＣで開催された米中首脳会談で得られた成
果と合意を履行するためのものと説明している。なお、トランプ米政権を巡っては、米連邦最高裁
判所が２月、トランプ米大統領による国際緊急経済権限法（IEEPA）を根拠とする関税措置につい
て、同法は大統領に関税を課す権限を認めていないとの判断を下したことを受けて、その後は通商
法 301 条に基づく関税で代替する方針を示している。中国当局は、トランプ米政権によるこうした
方針に留意していると表明するとともに、対抗措置として実施している 10％の追加関税は維持す
る方針を示している。とはいえ、米国からの輸入品に対する一部の追加関税の適用停止を継続する
ことは、米中間の経済・貿易協力にとって安定的で予見可能な政策環境の確保に資するとともに、
両国の経済・貿易関係の発展を促すとの見方を示している。 
米中関係を巡っては、今月、中国・深圳で開催される APEC（アジア太平洋経済協力）首脳会議に
合わせて、今年３回目となる首脳会談が行われる見通しである。さらに、来月、米国・マイアミで
開催予定のＧ20（主要 20 ヵ国・地域）首脳会合に合わせて、４回目の首脳会談が行われる可能性
もある。これらの首脳会談を前に「休戦」を演出することにより、９月の首脳会談で合意した内容
について詰めの協議を進めるための「時間稼ぎ」が可能になったと考えられる。また、中国では内
需が力強さを欠くなか、景気は外需への依存を一段と強めており、景気を下支えする観点からも、
外需を取り巻く環境の安定を維持したいとの思惑が影響した可能性がある。

【旺盛な輸出を背景に貿易黒字が積み上がる展開、貿易不均衡を巡る議論が再燃する可能性】 
このように当面は外需を取り巻く環境の安定が続くと見込まれるなか、10 月の輸出額は前年同月
比＋35.2％と前月（同＋36.3％）からわずかに鈍化したものの、引き続き高い伸びが続いているこ
とが示された。当研究所が試算した季節調整値に基づく前月比はわずかに減少しているものの、中
期的な基調は拡大傾向で推移するなど
底堅い動きが続いている（図１）。欧米諸
国などは、中国による貿易不均衡の是正
を求めて圧力を強めているにもかかわ
らず、米国向け（前年比＋32.9％）やＥ
Ｕ（欧州連合）向け（同＋24.4％）、英国
向け（同＋35.3％）はいずれも伸びが加
速している。さらに、米中摩擦が激化す
る背後で関係深化を図ってきた ASEAN
（東南アジア諸国連合）向け（前年比＋
46.3％）やインド向け（同＋43.7％）な
どアジア新興国向けは軒並み高い伸びが続いているほか、中南米向け（同＋32.1％）やアフリカ向
け（同＋19.5％）も堅調に推移している。ロシア向け（前年比＋31.9％）も高い伸びが続いており、
両国の経済的な結び付きが強まっている様子もうかがえる。世界的なＡＩ・半導体需要の旺盛さを
追い風に、韓国向け（前年比＋92.4％）や台湾向け（同＋61.8％）もともに加速している。ハイテ

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
ク製品に対する需要の旺盛さのみならず、中国製品全般に対する需要が堅調に推移していることも
輸出を押し上げる展開が続いている。 
一方で、10 月の輸入額は前年同月比＋41.6％と前月（同＋31.0％）から加速しているほか、前月
比も２ヵ月連続で拡大していると試算されるなど、旺盛な動きが続いている（図２）。ハイテク製
品のほか、イラン情勢の悪化による原油
高を受けて世界的に再エネやＥＶ（電気
自動車）需要が高まるなか、これらの生
産に必要なハイテク製品のほか、半導体
をはじめとする電子部品関連の輸入額
は軒並み高い伸びをみせている。加え
て、ハイテク製品の生産に不可欠である
銅をはじめとする鉱物資源関連の輸入
額も、市況の高止まりも重なる形で高い
伸びをみせており、原材料や素材・部材
の需要を活発化させている様子もうか
がえる。他方、内需が力強さを欠くなかで原油・石油製品、天然ガス、石炭などエネルギー資源の
輸入量はいずれも前年を下回る伸びで推移しているものの、市況の高止まりを反映して輸入額が押
し上げられている。 
この結果、10 月の貿易収支は＋1052.8 億ドルと前月（＋1335.3 億ドル）から黒字幅は縮小する
も、引き続き巨額の黒字を計上している。10 月までの累計の貿易黒字額は１兆 426 億ドルとなり、
2025 年に比べて１ヵ月早く１兆ドル超えを達成した。巨額の貿易黒字は人民元に対する需要を下
支えするとともに、先行きの人民元相場
の安定に寄与することが期待される。一
方で、足元では複数の銀行がドル建て預
金金利を引き上げる動きをみせており、
その背景には、一方向的な人民元高の進
展に対する当局の警戒感が影響してい
る可能性もある。足元では、人民元の通
貨バスケットは４年強ぶりの高水準で
推移しており（図３）、景気が外需への
依存を強めるなか、当局は人民元高によ
る輸出競争力の低下が景気の足かせと
なることを懸念しているとみられる。とはいえ、貿易黒字の拡大を受けて貿易不均衡を巡る世界的
な議論が再び強まることも予想され、中国当局にとっては難しい政策対応を迫られる局面が続くと
見込まれる。

3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，分別低於元大/市場預期 1~3 成 
3Q26 台幣營收 QoQ+241%、YoY+297%，主要受到 TRN3 大量出貨，整體
N3/N2 的營收占比從 47%跳增至 80%。惟受到量產營收佔比提高、部份專
案 NRE 遞延認列，使得本季毛利率季減 16.8 個百分點達 18%及 OPM 季減
7.6 個百分點達 13.9%，低於公司先前預期 2 個百分點，業外收益 4.53 億元、
EPS 37.67 元/季增 88%，獲利分別於元大/低於市場預期 1~3 成。 
4Q26 營收、獲利將創新高 
展望 4Q26 TRN3 持續大幅放量及新專案 tape out，營收、獲利持續創新高，
其中預期毛利率估計與 3Q26 接近，2026 全年營收估計落在 21~22 億美元、
毛利率>20%。元大預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+15%、YoY+536%、毛利率估計
18%、推估 EPS 43.17 元/季增 15%，較前次報告下修 15%，雖然上修營收
預估，但下修毛利率假設基礎。 
TRN3/4 推升 26~28 年營收達新高，第二家 CSP 具想像空間 
展望 2027 年預估營收有機會年增三位數，主要成長來自 TRN3 本身出貨倍
增及公司取得更高的量產配置外，2Q27 新增一家美系 CSP 客戶，估計訂單
規模達 10 億美元，細節不透露。下一代 TRN4 製程採 N2 GAA 的 Chiplet
架構、2.5D 封裝，公司負責所有 Die 的 physical design 與整合，包括
compute die 與 I/O die，整體 N2 專案的設計、整合及量產複雜度提高，估
計單價、潛在市場規模顯著超過 N3。中長期 CSP 由傳統晶片統包轉向
COT 分工模式，趨勢對公司有利。最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 118/186 元，
分別下修 15/上修 8%，係因 TRN3 出貨上修及新增 CSP 客戶而上修營收預
估，然毛利率表現低於公司先前指引而下修假設基礎。

元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，低於預期。展望 4Q26 TRN3 量能放大、新專案
tape out，營收、獲利將創歷史新高，推估 EPS 43.17 元。  
⧫  
展望 2027 年 AWS TRN3 仍為主要成長動能，除了 TRN3 本身出貨倍
增及公司量產配置提高，2Q27 新增第二家 CSP，將帶動營收有機會
年增三位數，TRN4 接續 27~28 年動能更為強勁。 
⧫  
最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 118/186 元，分別下修 15%/上修 8%，雖然
營收展望樂觀，然毛利率低於預期。維持買進評等、惟 27 年目標本益
比降至 25 倍，推得目標價 4600 元，儘管看好 TRN 3、4 帶動未來
2~3 年成長趨勢不變，然短期評價受系統性風險、籌碼面而有所下修。

TRN3/4 推升 26~28 年營收達新高，第二家 CSP 具想像空間 
展望 2027 年預估營收有機會年增三位數，主要成長來自 TRN3 本身出貨倍增及公司取得更高的量
產配置外，2Q27 新增一家美系 CSP 客戶，估計訂單規模達 10 億美元，細節不透露。下一代
TRN4 製程採 N2 GAA 的 Chiplet 架構、2.5D 封裝，公司負責所有 Die 的 physical design 與整合，
包括 compute die 與 I/O die，整體 N2 專案的設計、整合及量產複雜度提高，估計單價、潛在市場
規模顯著超過 N3。中長期 CSP 由傳統晶片統包轉向 COT 分工模式，趨勢對公司有利。最新預估
2026/27 年 EPS118/186 元，分別下修 15/上修 8%，係因 TRN3 出貨上修及新增 CSP 客戶而上修
營收預估，然毛利率表現低於公司先前指引而下修假設基礎。

Trainium 3 開始量產，推升 26 年營收倍數成長 
AWS 為公司最大客戶，營運表現與 AWS ASIC roadmap 連動性高，新一代 Trainium3 已於 2Q26
量產，受限於晶圓、封測產能吃緊，訂單排程已至 11 月，產品生命週期估計二年，根據供應鏈調
查，Trainium 3 出貨量估計約 200 萬顆，估計 2026 年 AWS 營收佔比達 7 成。下一代 Trainium 4
採台積 N2 製程，並且支援 NV Link Fusion 技術，擴大結合 GPU 生態系，客戶目標 2026 年 tape 
out、27 年底進入 prototype、28 年量產。除了 Trainium 以外，公司已取得多個 N2 設計專案，客
戶包括 HPC、北美 Networking，預期 N2 為下一波成長動能。 
圖 5： AWS AI 晶片 Roadmap

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估

Trainium 3 採用的 N3 製程與 HBM3E 高頻寬記憶體，使其在模型參數規模擴張下，提供更符合大
型訓練叢集需求的計算密度。與前代相比，Trainium 3 以更佳的運算效率與能耗比滿足雲端訓練的

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第4 頁，共 19頁  
成本結構要求，使 AI ASIC 在 AWS 內部的部屬比例持續提升。由於 Trainium 3 晶片邏輯面積、電
源網絡設計、時序收斂與封裝整合的難度均大幅高於前代，世芯在後段設計以及與台積電 CoWoS 
先進封裝流程的協同整合中扮演更為關鍵角色，主因後段設計與 DFT（Design-For-Test）複雜度
全面升級，反映製程推進至 N3 世代， ASIC 對高階設計服務供應商的依賴度進一步強化。 
展望 Trainium 4，其採用 N2 製程並首次導入 NVLink Fusion 技術，是 AWS 於 AI 訓練基礎架構上
的策略性變動。NVLink Fusion 使 Trainium 4 能與 NVIDIA GPU、CPU 及其他介面形成異質叢集
（heterogeneous cluster），並在跨晶片間實現高頻寬、低延遲的連結，讓 AWS 能以 ASIC + 
GPU 的混合架構（hybrid architecture）支撐大型模型訓練需求。此設計不僅能降低單純依賴 GPU 
所帶來的成本與供應鏈風險，也為 AWS Data Center 架構引入更高彈性，使 ASIC 不再僅限於推
論或中小型模型訓練，而成為可與 GPU 生態系共同運作的訓練加速器。此外 Trainium 4 的封裝規
格預期將導入更高能耗管理密度、更大晶片尺寸與更多 I/O die 整合，使物理設計與封裝協同需求
顯著增加，將使後段設計服務與 Turnkey 所需承擔的工程案量及技術門檻較 Trainium 3 提升。

圖 6： NVLink Fusion 架構示意

資料來源：NVIDIA Developer 
AWS 公布的架構方向顯示，其未來 AI 加速器的更新週期有望自過往 2.5–3 年縮短至 18–24 個月，
反映 LLM 訓練需求成長速度快於傳統半導體製程演進。AWS 持續提升自研 ASIC 在資料中心中占
比，並以 Trainium 系列與 GPU 共融的方式建立成本更具優勢的訓練叢集，使其 AI Infrastructure 
Roadmap 呈現加速態勢。觀察世芯過去營運成果可發現其營運高度連動於 AWS 專案，本中心預
期 2026 年將受惠於 Trainium 3 全面放量，並在 2027 年起隨 Trainium 4 的推出進入下一波量產高
峰。隨著 ASIC 在大型模型訓練的採用率提升與 AWS 從以 GPU 搭配 ASIC 轉向兩者並行的混合叢
集策略，預期世芯未來 2-3 年營運具備明確的擴張動能與高能見度。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第5 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
2025-2030 年全球 AI 晶片以 CAGR 32% 成長， AI/HPC 為主要動能 
近年 AI 應用擴大，初期 AI cloud 端訓練、推論採用通用型 GPU 為最大宗。隨 5G/AI/HPC 需求強
勁、晶圓代工廠持續往更先進製程邁進，未來將會有越來越多的 CSP/系統廠追求差異化、成本、
功耗考量而出現差異化晶片或自研晶片的需求，ASIC 應運而生，本中心預期隨著 ASIC 市場成長
加速，將帶動設計服務商營運。根據研調機構 Grand View Research，全球 AI 加速器市場將於
2030 年達約 2500 億美元，2025~30 年 CAGR 可達 32%，其中 ASIC CAGR 約 39%。由於
AI/HPC 晶片通常採用先進製程，其複雜性、生產時程及成本結構皆較適合採用 ASIC，此趨勢將推
動設計服務商成為 AI/HPC 晶片發展要角。根據供應鏈檢查，ASIC 對 CoWoS 的晶圓需求總量預
計將在 2026 年成長 85%，2027 年成長 71%，主要由 Google 的 TPU 以及 AWS 的 Trainium 系列
帶動成長。 
圖 7：AI 晶片市場 2025-30 年 CAGR 為 32% 圖 8：預計 2026/2027 年，ASIC 對 CoWoS 
的需求將增長 85%/71%

資料來源：公司資料、Grand View Research、元大投顧 資料來源：元大投顧預估

AI 晶片依功能可分為訓練/推論晶片 
AI 晶片在雲端進行訓練與推論，而在裝置端則著重推論功能，訓練晶片主要指通過大量資料輸入，
構建複雜深度學習類神經網路模型的 AI 晶片，運算能力較強；推論晶片主要是指利用訓練出來的
模型載入資料，計算並推理出各種結論的 AI 晶片。雲端與裝置端之算力需求有別，雲端的 AI 晶片
算力都要求在 30 TOPS 以上，而裝置端或邊緣端的 AI 晶片算力則在 5~30 TOPS 之間。本中心預
期隨 AI 持續發展，邊緣端 AI 應用的數量將不斷增加，帶動邊緣端 AI 晶片的需求持續上升，因此
在功耗、成本、大量生產的考量下，FPGA、ASIC 需求也將同步提升。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第6 頁，共 19頁  
圖 9：不同部署位置之 AI 晶片算力要求 圖 10：深度學習之訓練與推論示意圖

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、NVIDIA、元大投顧 
訓練端 AI 晶片以 GPU 為主流 
從技術架構來看 CPU、GPU 是較為成熟的通用型 AI 晶片，FPGA 和 ASIC 則是針對需求特化的半
訂製和全訂製晶片。因 AI 模型要求並行計算大量非結構化資料，CPU 在設計上以多工邏輯運算為
主要考量，較為適合處理複雜且不同類型之數據，而 GPU 則較為適合數據類型較為統一之邏輯運
算。GPU 構造相對單純，有數量眾多的計算單元與流水線，特別適合處理大量、類型統一的數據，
因此較為適合進行 AI 模型開發，預期 GPU 仍將是近年 AI 晶片架構之主流。

圖 11：CPU 與計算型 GPU 架構示意圖

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧

ASIC 將應用在裝置/邊緣端之推論 AI 晶片 
FPGA 優勢在於可隨著 AI 模型改變而進行調整，在產品出廠後仍可對其進行編程，以執行不同的
功能，這使得 FPGA 成為產品原型設計、驗證想法和少量生產的完美選擇但因價格昂貴，算力遠
低於 GPU，功耗表現、效能、成本皆不如 ASIC，故 FPGA 可視為 AI 晶片從 GPU 轉至 ASIC 中
間的過渡方案。ASIC 在設計初期就已限定將使用何種 AI 模型，ASIC 晶片設計時間較長，通常需
要 2~3 年，但是在生產成本、專用度、功耗等皆優於 GPU、FPGA，因此 GPU 主要用於雲端的訓
練、推論功能，而 ASIC 晶片多用於雲端或裝置端、邊緣端的推論 AI 晶片。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第8 頁，共 19頁  
AI 算力要求提高，訓練成本與耗時成為最大挑戰 
觀察現今 AI 產業對於算力的要求，其所需的算力提升遠高於摩爾定律，2012 年以來至今，AI 訓練
任務要求之算力每 3.43 個月便翻倍，大大超越晶片產業長期存在的摩爾定律 (每 18 個月晶片的性
能翻一倍)。因此 AI 訓練成本及消耗時間成新進者最大瓶頸，OpenAI 目前之 GPT-5 模型，訓練的
參數預估高達 2 兆個 (為 GPT-4 的 2 倍)，訓練成本可能來到 6,120 萬美元，若不考慮參數蒐集及
整理時間，光訓練人工智慧判讀資料，至少要花費 3-6 個月不等。

圖 13：人工智慧訓練成本高昂 圖 14：AI 模型訓練成本趨勢

資料來源：公司資料、The Next Platform、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、DataComony、元大投顧

ASIC 有效降低晶片單位生產成本、縮短製造時程 
ASIC 具計算效率高、單位製造成本低之特性，相比於其他晶片(CPU/GPU/FPGA)，ASIC 在計算
效率及功耗上表現更為優異，單位製造成本也較低，然而初期開發成本較其他標準品高。隨著製程
技術不斷演進，投資成本也急遽增加，IC 設計公司對於採用先進製程的產品開發上也傾向分工，將
後段晶片布局與實現交由專業的設計服務業者，如此不僅能有效降低研發成本，亦能縮短製造時程；
此外，近年來系統廠為了追求時效性與差異化，開始自行設計與規劃關鍵晶片，此亦為設計服務業
帶來龐大商機。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第9 頁，共 19頁  
ASIC 可針對客戶需求打造客製化目的晶片，其算力使用效率將較標準產品 GPU 更貼近客戶需求。
由每單位成本的算力來觀察，ASIC 晶片仍具有較佳的性價比，將對於裝置、邊緣端的推論產品具
有較大之經濟效益；而總算力強大的 GPU 則更適用於需要長時間、大規模之平行運算之雲端訓練。 
近年國際系統廠傾向以自研晶片取代標準品，CSP 資本支出維持高檔 
在 AI 大趨勢下，預期超大規模業者的內部晶片開發將進一步加速，並將更多研發資源分配給 CPU、
GPU、ASIC 和 FPGA 等關鍵 IC，因為上述 IC 是達成設備效能和滿足自身需求之關鍵，對於超大
規模業者來說，產品的差異化以及產品上市時間為超越對手的重要關鍵。基於此因素，眾多國際大
廠正在開發自製伺服器晶片，以達成客製化效能或需求、縮減晶片成本，並降低對 Nvidia 的依賴、
強化對 AI 加速器的議價能力。一般伺服器和 AI 伺服器的需求正不斷成長，超大規模企業直接與 IP
廠商合作開發指令集，利用設計服務或 IP 公司來協助晶片設計和整合代工資源，接著再將晶片生
產外包給晶圓代工廠和封測廠。

獲利調整與股票評價  
維持買進、目標本益比降至 25 倍，基於短期系統風險、籌碼影響評價面 
3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，低於元大預期 25%，其中 TRN3 ramp 速度低於先前預期，使得營收動能低
於先前公司預期的 1 成。本季毛利率約 18%、OPM 13.9%，分別低於公司預期 20~25%、high 
teens，主因為量產營收佔比提升、部份 NRE 專案遞延認列等。展望 4Q26 公司預期營收、獲利皆
會創新高，其中估計營收有機會季增>1 成、毛利率與 3Q26 接近，推估 EPS 43.17 元。預估 2026
全年營收 YoY 成長 120%，其中估計 TRN3 營收佔比>7 成。展望 27 年公司預估營收 YoY 約達三
位數成長，主要動能來自 TRN3 本身出貨量倍增及公司供應比提升，加上 2Q27 新增 CSP 客戶可
望開始逐季貢獻收，營收展望優於普遍市場預估 YoY 成長 6~7 成。下一代 TRN4 採台積 N2 製程
Chiplet 架構，與上代差異在於支援 NV Link Fusion 互連技術，整合 GPU 生態系市場、應用更具彈
性，有利於 AWS 市 場 規 模 擴 展 。 目 前 世 芯 亦 參 與 Compute die/IO/Memory controller 相關
Backend design service，惟效益在 28 年。中長期產業趨勢包括 AI 應用擴大、ASIC 委外需求強勁、
CSP 走向 COT 分工模式及先進封裝重要性提升等仍將持續推升公司營運向上不變。最新預估
2026/27 年 EPS 分別為 118/186 元，分別下修 15%/上修 8%，雖然上修營收預估、但下修毛利率
假設基礎，主因 TRN3 供應比提升、新增 CSP 客戶帶動營收動能優於預期，然而毛利率受到量產
佔比提升而表現低於預期，因而下修 2~3 個百分點。目前根據公司 2026/27 年預估獲利換算本益
比分別為 24/15 倍，其中以 2026 年本益比落於公司近一年本益比區間 20-55 倍下緣，低於國內同
業平均本益比約 73 倍及國外同業平均本益比 33 倍。維持買進，惟目標本益比由 35 倍降至 25 倍、
2027 年 EPS 為評價基礎，推得目標價 4600 元，雖然看好 AI 市場需求大爆發及主力客戶展望佳、
趨勢更顯有利於世芯中長線營運動能，惟反映短期股價受系統性風險、籌碼因素而使評價面有所下
修。

公司簡介  
世芯電子（Alchip Technologies, 3661 TT）成立於 2003 年，為專注於高階 ASIC（Application-Specific Integrated Circuit）
設計服務的領導廠商，核心能力涵蓋從前段邏輯設計，到後段實作、先進封裝整合（HBM/CoWoS），以及量產代工管理
（Turnkey）。公司長期與台積電合作，聚焦 7nm、5nm、3nm 甚至未來 2nm 等先進製程，是全球少數能在先進節點承接
大型 HPC / AI ASIC 的專業設計服務商。世芯的商業模式以專案導向的 NRE（Non-Recurring Engineering）與量產導向的 
Turnkey 兩大事業構成。NRE 收入反映設計階段的工程複雜度及製程難度，隨 ASIC 需求向先進製程演進而快速成長；
Turnkey 則整合晶圓投片、封測、驗證到客戶出貨等所有供應鏈流程，近年在大型雲端服務商導入自研 AI 加速器的趨勢下，
已成為公司營收與獲利增長的主軸。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第16 頁，共 19頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 世芯整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於平均的位置，
但在 IP 行業中的公司中排名均於同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 世芯的整體曝險屬於中等水準，但略低於 IC 設計行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的 ESG 議
題包含人力資本、商業道德及資源使用等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：世芯在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。世芯已任命一個管理委員會
來監督 ESG 問題，雖說公司有針對重大 ESG 議題進行管控，但對於吹哨者保護機制較為薄弱。公司對重大 ESG 議題
的整體管理水準處於平均水準。

附錄：重要揭露事項

買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。

動態更新 散熱 ‧ 台灣

奇鋐 
(3017.TW/3017 TT)

液冷趨勢持續推升 2027-28年 EPS成長 
重要訊息  
奇鋐公布 3Q26 EPS  29.32 元，大致符合預期。我們預期 4Q26 營收將季
增，且隨著液冷業務持續擴張，2027 年成長展望正向。 
評論及分析 
3Q26 EPS 符合預期，利潤率優於預估。3Q26 EPS 為 29.32 元 (季增
20%、年增 117%),符合凱基預估,並較市場共識高出 3%。毛利率 33.9% 
及營業利益率 28.5% 均優於凱基預估,因產品組合改善及生產效率提升,惟遭
3.04 億元匯兌損失抵銷,3Q26 EPS 大致符合預期。伺服器業務為 3Q26 營收
及毛利率成長的主要動能,營收年增 84%,營收占比 66%。另一方面,3C 及富
世達 (6805 TT,NT$1,990 ,增加持股) 營收亦年增 20-30%,分別因 Apple (美 ) 
iPhone 及 iPad 超薄 VC (均熱板) 以及快接頭 (QD) 出貨成長帶動。1Q-3Q26 
伺服器營收年增 122%,帶動 EPS 年增 129%至 73.86 元,同時伴隨利潤率擴
大。凱基預期上述成長動能將持續為 4Q26 至 2027 年 EPS 成長動能。 
液冷由 GPU 擴展至 ASIC，由 AI 伺服器延伸至通用型伺服器，並由運算端
擴大至網通傳輸端。Nvidia (美) GB300 與 VR200 AI GPU 的設計全面採用水
冷板模組,且預期 HGX 及 CPU 機櫃亦將轉向液冷設計。隨著 Helios、
Trainium 及 TPU 機櫃採用液冷,且 CPU、通用型伺服器、交換器 (1.6T)、電
源供應器、光晶片及光收發模組的液冷採用率提升,液冷 TAM 成長將使奇鋐
受惠。為滿足更高的需求,奇鋐正於越南擴充液冷產能,涵蓋水冷板、歧管、
機殼及冷卻液分配單元 (CDU),2027 年將擴增逾 50%。而液冷設計於 2027 
年在 AI 伺服器應用上，雙面水冷板出貨將增加,用於 ASIC 晶片設計。此外,
水冷板加入鑽石銅等新材料或縮小流道,也可能是提升散熱效能的潛在趨勢,
將提高 ASP。奇鋐在所有主要美系 CSP,以及多數企業客戶與 neocloud 的液
冷供應上居領先地位，因此預期其液冷營收將於 2027-28 年大幅成長逾 
50%，基於產能與 ASP 擴增。加計機殼因需求與產能成長帶動，營收成
長，凱基預期奇鋐 2027 年伺服器營收將年增 65-70%,優於公司整體營收成
長，使伺服器營收占比由 2026 年的 65-70% 提升至 2027 年的 70-75%。
在產品組合改善下,預期 2027 年毛利率及營業利益率將擴大。奇鋐指引 
2026 年資本支出為 150 億元，2027 年為 150-200 億元。此外，公司為
iPhone/iPad 超薄 VC 主要供應商，將推升 3C 營收成長，使該業務營收占比
由 2026 年的 4-5% 升至 2027 年的 8-10%。凱基看好奇鋐營收與 EPS 的
上升趨勢，微調 2026-27 年 EPS 預估至 106.63 元及 173.39 元。 
投資建議 
凱基維持奇鋐「增加持股」評等，並將目標價調整為 4,335 元，隱含 25 倍
2027 年本益比，反映 AI 伺服器液冷產品需求進入上升週期。 
投資風險 
AI 伺服器迭代時程延遲、新台幣與人民幣升值、原料成本攀升。

鋐伺服器業務營收成長。 
3. 因應 AI 伺服器需求強勁，奇鋐將在
越南擴充水冷板、歧管與機殼產能。

圖 12: 公司概況  圖 13: 1-3Q26散熱模組與機殼業務合計營收占比於提升至 85% 
奇鋐成立於 1991 年，為 3C(佔 2025 年整體營收 14%)、伺服
器(52%)與網通(7%)及其他(2%)的散熱解決方案供應商。公司
散熱產品包含散熱模組、風散與均熱片 (VC)。散熱業務 (模組
與風扇) 佔 2025 年營收 57%。奇鋐其他產品包含機殼與機櫃 
( 佔 2025 年整體營收 18% )與系統組裝與製造介面周邊
(16%)，軸承(9%)則來自子公司富世達 (6805 TT, NT$ 1,990, 
增加持股)。

### Page 1

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

法說會重點摘要

益安 
(6499 TT, NT$73, 增加持股)

重點摘要 
1. 益安主力產品 UroCross（BPH 暫時性植入物）於 10M26 在美國德
州完成首例商業化手術，台灣許可證亦於 11M26 初取得。美國每
療程保險給付約 8,000-1 萬美元，出廠價約 6,000 美元，定價與
UroLift 相近；前期由公司自行推廣並控制費用，營收預期緩步向
上、不會立即大幅跳升 
2. 授權方面，公司傾向以 UroCross 子公司股權交易方式整體出售，
參考同業成交價約 3-6 億美元，目前與數家固定對象深入洽談，對
方未要求須先達一定銷量。Duett 樞紐試驗需收案約 72 例、目前
收案 30 多例，FDA 限制單一醫院收案數使進度放慢，公司表示不
一定要收完案才授權 
3. CDMO 為「益安 2.0」的現金流主軸，2Q26 營收新台幣 1.07 億
元，3Q26 毛利率約 20%、接近損益兩平；合併現金約新台幣 8-9
億元、年費用約 6 億元，2027 年費用預期持續收斂。我們認為，
隨量產專案增加，CDMO 毛利率有望朝公司所稱成熟期 40-50%靠
攏，搭配研發費用收斂，可逐步縮小本業虧損 
結論 
我們認為 UroCross 已完成美國首例商業化手術，並具備明確給付碼與
接近 UroLift 的定價，商業化驗證已到位，有利授權談判；資產切分至
子公司後可採股權交易，結構較當年 Cross-Seal 的資產交易單純。產
品 6 個月後取出、不留金屬植入物，正對應永久植入物因移除需求而
銷售封頂的痛點，是吸引 BPH 領域大廠的核心差異化。近期驗證點為
美國醫院擴點速度與授權洽談進展，我們看好授權落地後可為益安挹
注資金，支撐 CDMO 整合與新專案布局

UroCross 商業化進度 
 美國：取證後啟動前期商業化，從取證到可入院手術一般約需半年，
10M26 初於德州醫院完成首例商業化手術；後續逐步擴展至更多
醫院與手術中心，讓更多醫師完成認證 
 台灣：8M26 邀請美國 KOL 來台分享，讓台灣醫師先熟悉產品；許
可證已於 11M26 初取得，後續將陸續展開商業化活動 
 製造與給付： 
 製造由集團 CDMO 事業的美國子公司 Medeologix 負責，已開始
備料並進行量產可製造性優化，未來量大時傾向移回台灣生產以
降低成本 
 已與 KOL 及美國泌尿科醫學會溝通醫院可採用的給付碼，公司
表示進度順利

### Page 2

台灣 益安

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

 推廣策略： 
 前期美國與台灣皆由公司自行推廣，逐一陪同醫師操作數例，確
保病人選擇與執行方式符合原設計，不採一次全面鋪貨 
 推廣費用採控制方式，公司人力不大幅增加，美國搭配 1099 外
部約聘業務，目前未規劃組建百人銷售團隊 
 營收預期緩步向上、不會立即大幅跳升，但因單價相對好，不至
於要到 10 年後才達到市場期待的目標

定價與給付 
 美國：有明確的保險給付碼與價格，每療程保險給付約 8,000-1 萬
美元；UroCross 出廠價約為保險給付的 6 成、約 6,000 美元，讓醫
院保有獲利空間，初期實際售價依各醫院採購議價有高有低 
 與 UroLift 比較：UroLift 牌價每支 900-950 美元，每次需對稱植入
4-6 支，出給醫院約 4,500-5,000 美元，兩者定價相近 
 台灣：大部分醫材剛上市時健保不太會給付，預期先以自費方式上
市，納入健保可能需 3-5 年；台灣私人保險較多，與醫院、醫師仍
需溝通，定價尚未定案 
 療效：UroCross 植入 6 個月後取出，目前追蹤 3 年效果仍維持，資
料至少涵蓋 42 個月

授權規劃 
 交易形式： 
 最終目標為授權或出售，公司最希望整體 buyout，需靠多方競
爭達成；集團傾向出售子公司，參考同業成交價約 3-6 億美元 
 過往案例的其他形式包括 global licensing deal 、大廠先投資並
取得經銷權與 right of first re fusal，或投資加 option right 與部
分經銷權；Olympus 收購 Medi-Tate 即先投資取得 option right ，
之後執行全數收購 
 一直有大廠來談獨家經銷權，公司認為尚未到該階段，希望爭取
更好的條件 
 交易結構： 
 UroCross 已切分至子公司，可做股權交易，最為乾淨，後續款
項仍可另行設計；益安持股約 80%，其餘由當初貢獻構想的醫
師持有 
 當年 Cross-Seal 掛在益安本體下，只能以資產交易出售給
Terumo，之後還需以複雜的 service agreement 支援後續開發；
有了該次經驗，各產品皆切分至不同子公司

### Page 3

台灣 益安

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 3

 洽談進度： 
 目前有數家固定洽談對象，包括已有 BPH 產品線及在泌尿科著
墨較多的公司，雙方已簽署相關程序、談得相當深入，惟公司無
法透露進度 
 對方正進行 DD、諮詢 KOL，可能要求觀摩手術，之後再上呈高
層決策，過程與當年洽談 Terumo 案類似；潛在對象未要求
UroCross 須先達一定銷量 
 公司未給授權時程預估，目標越快越好，前提是條件須為股東所
接受 
 授權區域：以全球為目標，但希望保留台灣及部分亞洲區域；國際
大廠推廣時台灣多排第 3-4 順位，上市好幾年後才用得到，且大中
華區不一定每家大廠都想要。除台灣與美國外，暫無其他國家取證
規劃

競品比較 
 UroLift：年銷售高峰約 3-4 億美元；為永久植入物，取出須做破壞
性較大的手術，上市數年後醫師常接到移除需求、裝置意願下降，
銷售因此封頂 
 ProVerum、Zenflow：皆為永久植入物，由未上市新創公司自行銷
售；Zenflow 前陣子獲 Cook Medical 投資並取得經銷權，部分金額
為借款，公司表示這也是會考慮的合作方式 
 iTind：需放置 7 天，病患反映疼痛明顯、須服用大量止痛藥，醫師
使用意願低 
 能量類手術：TURP、Rezum、Aquablation 皆為不可逆的組織挖除
或消融，可能傷及神經 
 UroCross 差異化：設計為 6 個月後取出，體內不留金屬植入物，後
續磁振造影或癌症篩檢不受遮擋；定位於藥物治療（頭暈等副作用）
與手術（恢復期長、不可逆）之間的治療缺口 
 自行銷售路線：公司以 PROCEPT 為例，其自行銷售並 IPO，市值約
11 億美元，但至今仍虧損，主因 Sales & Marketing 費用高且需投
放設備；UroCross 為純耗材，ROE 理論上較佳

Duett（胸主動脈修復） 
 樞紐試驗進度：取證需收案約 72 例，目前收案 30 多例，近幾個月
增加不多；FDA 限制每家醫院收案 5 例，須開設更多醫院，3Q26
新增數家醫院後收案才陸續增加。目前約 5-10 家醫院，將先收滿
一輪再視需要增加；病患多為急性送醫且須簽署同意書，收案較不
易，安全性方面尚無 SAE 
 試驗設計：single arm 、不需解盲，主要終點為 30 天中風、死亡等
重大不良事件，與歷史手縫資料比較；手術時間縮短未列入終點，

### Page 4

台灣 益安

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 4

僅做紀錄。先前 20 例無重大不良事件，FDA 因此同意延伸為樞紐
試驗 
 產品定位： 
 主動脈剝離置換手術須停止循環，血流中斷最好不超過 30 分鐘；
通往腦部的 3 條血管手縫每條約 15 分鐘，使用 Duett 每條約 1-
2 分鐘，可降低中風與下肢血栓風險，且縫合處不滲血 
 美國每年手術量約 2-3 萬例，亞洲更多，大中華區發生率較歐美
高；產品具完整專利保護 
 授權與競爭： 
 已有一些授權討論，公司表示不一定要收完案才授權 
 最接近的競品為 Terumo 自行開發的 RapidLink，剛開始臨床，
Terumo 亦為潛在授權對象 
 CryoLife（現 Artivion）曾以約 2 億美元收購一款主動脈剝離產
品，惟其為置於升主動脈的網狀支架，與 Duett 處理的 3 條血管
接合並非同類問題；Duett 資產同樣放在子公司底下

CDMO 
 策略定位：「益安 2.0」以 CDMO 建立穩定現金流，CDMO 與創新
醫材為兩大主軸；除 Terumo 款項外，營收皆來自 CDMO，台灣與
美國皆有據點 
 營收：2Q26 營收新台幣 1.07 億元，不含 Terumo 款項與授權金 
 客戶結構：約 2/3 客戶已進入臨床試量產階段，另有固定量產客戶；
醫材一旦選定製造廠，更換有一定難度。即將放量的重要客戶皆為
美國的心血管介入產品創新公司，募資能力佳 
 毛利率與獲利：3Q26 毛利率約 20%，仍在成長階段，更多專案量
產後可望改善；目前接近損益兩平、小幅虧損。公司表示醫材
CDMO 成熟時毛利率至少 40-50%，大型 CDMO 如 Integer、
Nordson 約 30-40%至 70%

財務與資本規劃 
 現金與費用：合併帳上現金約新台幣 8-9 億元；年費用約 6 億元，
因先前臨床試驗而偏高。2027 年研發費用持平或略減、銷售費用
略增，整體持續收斂，惟尚無明確預估 
 股權結構：晟德原持股約 28-29%，釋出部分持股後目前約 20%出
頭，益安仍為其權益法投資公司，短期內不會改變 
 資金規劃：2025 年底辦理增資，價格 90 元，短期無資金需求；若
授權成功，資金可能用於 CDMO 上下游與平行整合，或以投資、
育成方式參與新專案

Document 669: 华尔街与硅谷强推Anthropic上市：一场“大而不能败”的AI保卫战

Source PDF: all report pdf/华尔街与硅谷强推Anthropic上市：一场“大而不能败”的AI保卫战.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

22 家顶级投⾏联合承销，英伟达、微软等七⼤科技巨头承诺上市后⽴即注资 260 亿美元。
在 AI 泡沫忧虑与⻩仁勋辞职的双重阴影下，这场 " 太关键、不能失败 " 的资本盛事，正考验整
个科技⽣态系统对 AI 未来的信仰底线。
摘要：
Anthropic 正在冲刺⼀场可能创下史上最⼤规模 IPO 纪录的资本市场盛事，⽽推动这场上市的，
不仅是华尔街的逐利本能，更是整个科技⽣态系统对 AI 未来的集体押注。
Anthropic 本周披露计划以最⾼ 1.4 万亿美元估值融资最多 1000 亿美元。公司已联合 22 家承销⾏
展开路演，同时获得包括英伟达、微软、博通在内的七家科技巨头承诺在上市后⽴即合计注资
260 亿美元。前美国银⾏股票资本市场全球负责⼈ Craig Coben 将此次 IPO 定性为 " 太关键、不能
失败 " （ too crucial to fail ）。
此次上市并⾮⼀帆⻛顺。 AI 基础设施过度扩张的债务隐忧已令股债市场剧烈震荡数周，英伟达
CEO ⻩仁勋的突然辞职进⼀步加剧市场不安。尽管如此， Anthropic 联合创始⼈兼 CEO Dario

Amodei 在路演中刻意呈现出⼀种泰然⾃若的姿态 ——" 我们不怎么纠结于具体在哪个⽉、哪⼀
天、什么市场环境下上市， " 他在摩根⼤通总部 56 楼对 Jamie Dimon 表⽰。
估值与阵容：⼀次史⽆前例的资本动员
Anthropic 此次 IPO 的规模超出以往任何⼀宗上市交易。公司寻求的 1000 亿美元融资额和最⾼ 1.4
万亿美元估值，均有望刷新历史纪录—— 尽管这已较银⾏家们在夏季放⻛的 2 万亿美元估值有所
下调，原因正是市场情绪在过去数⽉明显降温。
以⾼盛和摩根⼠丹利为⾸的 22 家承销⾏正在紧锣密⿎地推进路演。摩根⼤通和花旗负责主导⾯
向散⼾投资者的销售⼯作， Anthropic 预计散⼾将占此次发⾏规模的约 20% 。根据 Evercore 分析
师测算，各参与银⾏合计有望从本次交易中收取约 4 亿美元承销费⽤。
更引⼈注⽬的是机构端的阵容。七家科技巨头 —— 包括英伟达、微软、博通 —— 已承诺在
Anthropic 上市后⽴即合计投⼊ 260 亿美元。值得注意的是，这七家公司不仅部分已是 Anthropic

的现有股东，全部也都是其付费客⼾。 Anthropic 上市成功，意味着这些公司将拥有⼀个更稳
定、更持续的服务采购⽅。这种利益捆绑，使这七家科技公司成为此次 IPO 最有动⼒的 " 护盘
⽅ " 之⼀。
被动资⾦与对冲需求：投资者的 " 不得不买 " 逻辑
除了主动投资者，⼀股看不⻅的⼒量同样在为 Anthropic 保驾护航。由于公司估值体量庞⼤，叠
加近期允许⼤型 IPO 更快纳⼊主要股指的新规， Anthropic 上市初期将⾃动获得被动指数基⾦和
ETF 数百亿美元的资⾦流⼊。这种机制在主动型投资者中制造出⼀种 " 不能错过 " 的⼼理压⼒ ——
持有 Anthropic ，本质上是在与⼤盘基准对⻬。
Irving Investors 创始⼈ Jeremy Abelson 将 Anthropic 描述为 " 必须持有 " 的标的，理由是 " 它是对现
有投资组合的⼀种对冲 " 。这⼀逻辑的背景在于： Anthropic 此前的产品发布公告有时已令软件板
块股价承压，意味着不持有 Anthropic 的投资者实际上⾯临着来⾃这家公司的结构性⻛险敞⼝。

财富管理公司 PCM Encore 创始⼈ Michael Paulus 则对这笔投资给出了更坦率的概率式评估：
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
140 收藏 分享到 :
路演叙事：克制⻛格下的商业野⼼
在路演呈现⽅式上， Anthropic 刻意选择了⼀条与 Elon Musk 旗下 SpaceX 今年 6 ⽉ IPO 截然不同的
路径。 SpaceX 路演视频以管弦乐开场，画⾯扫过⽉球，配合 Musk 改变⼈类命运的宏⼤宣⾔；承
销银⾏更在⼤厅搭建⽕箭模型、供应太空主题餐⻝。
Anthropic 的路演视频则由 Dario Amodei 穿着⼀件⻘绿⾊⽑⾐出镜，开场⼏分钟内便警告 AI 存
在 " 制造⽣化武器或发动⽹络攻击 " 的⻛险 —— 尽管他随后也将 AI 称为 " ⼈类历史上最具前景的技
术 " ，并表⽰相信 AI 将在⼗年内帮助终结癌症和精神疾病。摩根⼤通路演活动的伴⼿礼，也不过
是⽪质笔记本电脑包和印有 "Anthropic" 字样的曲奇饼⼲。

在内容层⾯， Anthropic ⾼管在私下会⾯中向投资者重点传达的信息是：公司将利⽤ IPO 募集资
⾦ ⾃ 研 芯 ⽚ 、 ⾃ 建 数 据 中 ⼼ ， 主 动 掌 控 ⾃ ⾝ 命 运 ， 以 求 在 市 场 波 动 中 保 持 独 ⽴ 性 。 Dario
Amodei 还在多场投资者会议中⽤相当篇幅介绍公司在新药研发和⾃动化科学实验室领域的早期
探索 —— 尽管据两位与会投资者透露，这⼀⽅向⽬前尚⽆明显商业进展。
噪⾳未散， IPO 窗⼝仍待验证
Anthropic 选择在并不理想的市场窗⼝强⾏推进上市。 AI 基础设施⼤规模举债扩张所引发的投资
者担忧，已在过去数周持续冲击股市和债市。⽽本周英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋的辞职，更为整个 AI ⽣
态系统的未来投资前景蒙上了新的阴影。
Craig Coben 在谈及当前局势时表⽰， " ⽬前市场认为 AI ⽣态系统能够承受⻩仁勋辞职的冲击，
但对于这⼀切意味着什么、将如何影响未来的⽀出和投资，市场仍在实时消化之中。 "
下周，⾸席财务官 Krishna Rao 与公司总裁、 Dario Amodei 之姐 Daniela Amodei 将赴⾼盛与投资

者会⾯，路演进⼊最后冲刺阶段。在摩根⼤通 56 楼的活动结束时，该⾏资产与财富管理业务负
责⼈ Mary Erdoes 向在场⼈⼠重申了她从 Dario Amodei 演讲中提炼出的核⼼信息：
" 未来⼗年， Anthropic 某个时点股价腰斩七成？⼤概率会发⽣。但它也可能涨⾄当前价值的⼗
倍，同样⼤概率是真的。 "
" ⽬标是少承诺、多兑现。我们真⼼希望他们能做到。 "

央行发布三季度货币政策执行报告 
市场分析 
宏观流动性： 
（01）美联储：12月加息概率83.5 %，较昨日+5.1个百分点。 
（02）美元流动性：美元指数102.2，较昨日+0.6点；20日均值较昨日+0.0点，5日均值较昨日+0.1点。比特币74935.2
点，较昨日+872.3点；20日均值较昨日+488.2点，5日均值较昨日-1044.3点。 
（03）货币流动性：美元SOFR/FFR利差20日均值-8.7BP，较昨日+0.1BP；5日均值-8.0BP，较昨日-1.4BP。人民币
DR007/OMO007利差20日均值-3.4BP，较昨日-0.3BP；5日均值-4.0BP，较昨日+1.4BP。

宏观经济： 
（04）商品市场：铜金比价20日均值3.51盎司/吨，较昨日+0.00；5日均值3.41盎司/吨，较昨日-0.03。油金比价20
日均值2.65盎司/100桶，较昨日-0.02；5日均值2.40盎司/100桶，较昨日-0.08。 
（05）债券市场：美债期限利差20日均值33.6BP，较昨日-0.8BP；5日均值32.4BP，较昨日-1.8BP。中债期限利差
20日均值41.4BP，较昨日-0.1BP；5日均值39.9BP，较昨日-0.2BP。德债期限利差20日均值35.5BP，较昨日-0.5BP；
5日均值32.4BP，较昨日-0.8BP。 
（06）股票市场：美股同比9.8%，较昨日+1.6个百分点；A股同比-12.4%，较昨日+1.2个百分点；欧股同比10.0%，
较昨日+1.2个百分点。

美国经济： 
（07）经济预期：经济意外指数20日均值49.9，较昨日+1.7；5日均值64.9，较昨日+3.6。 
（08）金融条件：金融条件指数20日均值-1.8，较昨日+0.1；5日均值-1.5，较昨日+0.1。

中国经济： 
（09）经济预期：经济意外指数20日均值0.3，较昨日+2.1；5日均值7.3，较昨日-0.3。 
（10）碳排成本：CEA碳排放权20日均值95.4，较昨日-0.4；5日均值93.0，较昨日-0.9。 
（11）运输成本：内贸集装箱运价指数1027.0，较上周+0.98%，较去年同期-15.54%。外贸集装箱进口运价指数117.5，
较上周-0.53%，较去年同期+17.55%。外贸集装箱出口运价指数161.1，较上周+2.28%，较去年同期+81.89%。 
（12）房地产：30城地产周度成交199.22万平，较上周-28.10%，较去年同期-10.11%。30城地产周度成交5周均值
220.97万平，较上周-4.11%，较去年同期-13.90%。

策略 
美国中期选举后的市场仍然波动，等待APEC峰会三方会晤的风险管控可能。美元短期内在美债利率的强势（非农
给出经济强，通胀给出加息预期强）叠加非美地缘压力状态下维持强势特征，峰会前仍带来一定压力。央行发布
三季度货币政策报告，显示适度宽松的货币政策基调不变。

◼ 研究范围：基于日频拥挤度趋势因子数据库，研究覆盖198只大盘及行
业指数、6150只A股个股和1701只ETF，并将顶底识别与交易胜率两
套方法扩展至港股，比较不同资产层级与市场的信号表现。 
◼ 信号与交易规则：围绕风险度 TR、短中期动量及综合评分等指标，构
建8类超跌抄底与9类高位追涨信号。信号于收盘后确认、次日收盘成
交，固定持有5、10、20或60个交易日，并以回撤8%、最长持有120
个交易日的移动止盈作为补充。 
◼ 分组筛选与滚动检验：指数按风格或行业小类、个股按“市值档×行业”、
ETF按小类合并交易样本，在滚动10年窗口内按前7年训练、后3年
验证筛选信号，另以逐标的历史统计和每日状态支持具体跟踪。 
回测表现：不同资产层级的胜率与信号构成存在差异 
◼ 指数层级越细，入选组合验证胜率越低：1995—2026年32个滚动锚点
共得到9573条有效组合，训练段与验证段胜率中位数分别为66.2%和
63.6%。大盘及风格指数、申万一级、申万二级的验证段胜率中位数依
次为65.9%、64.3%和61.6%。 
◼ A股个股以超跌类组合为主：全A个股共得到32438条有效组合，其
中超跌抄底类占82.0%，高于指数体系的50.4%。四档市值的验证段胜
率中位数为60.0%—60.7%，档间差异较小，且整体低于指数层面。 
◼ ETF整体训练与验证差距较小：2012—2026年共得到811条有效组合，
超跌抄底类占68.2%。其中验证样本不少于3条的615条组合，训练段
与验证段胜率中位数为65.5%和65.0%；风格与规模指数类验证段分别
为66.7%和66.2%，跨境与行业指数类为61.9%和60.0%。 
◼ 港股交易信号需按自身结果筛选：港股有效组合的训练段与验证段胜率
中位数为63.2%和57.1%，A股对照为64.3%和60.0%。港股固定持有
60个交易日的验证段胜率中位数为62.5%，高于其余固定持有期限；具
体选择仍需结合收益、盈亏比和样本量。 
最新观点：信号状态以持有为主，ETF超跌类信号较为集 
◼ 指数与个股以既有信号跟踪为主：截至2026年9月30日，指数体
系共有306条信号状态记录，其中304条为持有；全A个股共有2337
条持有记录，覆盖个股的各市值档均无当日新触发买入记录。该截面主
要反映前期信号延续，后续重点跟踪持有信号是否触发到期或移动止盈
条件。 
◼ ETF信号集中于超跌修复：截至2026年10月15日，ETF共有644条
信号状态记录，涉及164只ETF，其中642条处于持有状态。超跌抄底
类占97.2%，深双低、双低与极限抄底合计489条，占75.9%。后续可
重点观察相关标的的价格修复及信号退出情况，并结合各类 ETF的历
史验证结果判断。 
◼ 风险提示：模型基于历史数据构建，存在失效风险；宏观数据存在发布
滞后与修订；海外政策、地缘冲突等不可控外力可能导致模型短期失效。
